### q_company_c5fcc97bfb2974073676415c_targeted: "Bosch (Automotive Division)" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- President Trump has implemented a 25% tariff on foreign-made auto parts, including powertrain components, seats, and airbags, while introducing some key exemptions to reduce the strain on automakers. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_030ef6fab6c8784dfaff1fab`, `chunk_9f82b7462f46a2f16899791a`).
- Bosch workers discuss electronic stability control product at the company's facility in South Carolina. ([source](https://www.autonews.com/manufacturing/suppliers/an-supplier-tariffs-relationships-0728), `claim_6a19b706f46a2773bff05f1f`, `chunk_b68a512287e24d0e157a7e99`).
- The White House announced that passenger vehicles, certain trucks, and auto parts will be exempt from the new 10% global tariff set to take effect on February 24, 2026. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_5660f160c83adcce52497d18`, `chunk_e8a1ffd62f7b512ed56a6a50`).
- The U.S. government has confirmed the implementation of a 15 percent tariff on European Union (EU) autos and auto parts, effective retroactively from August 1, 2025. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_ce71e21636d34271fdf51af5`, `chunk_a58187ff4611e6159670dd83`).
- President Donald Trump announced a 25 percent tariff on all medium- and heavy-duty trucks imported into the United States, set to take effect on November 1, 2025. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_c634da232ee0fb8e829413b2`, `chunk_a58187ff4611e6159670dd83`).

### q_company_ae431317033c698ebc8dbdc9_targeted: "Bridgestone Bandag" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Section 232 metals tariffs apply to auto components: after the April 6, 2026 restructure, articles wholly of steel/aluminum/copper carry 50% while derivative components substantially made of those metals carry 25% ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_009c7e0b9d5a93dcd1495cec`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- EU-built vehicles and auto parts fall under the EU-US trade deal's flat 15% all-inclusive rate, which applies in lieu of the 27.5% Section 232 auto regime. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_8fbf1da80f96e1c2a94540d4`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).

### q_company_9f7649ab93e553fce03a0293_targeted: "Club Car LLC" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- If the Department of Commerce and the U.S. International Trade Commission (USITC) rule in favor of this request, duties may apply retroactively, comprising shipments made up to 90 days before a final ruling is made. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_0acf58eb4bdae7687e4ad6f2`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- The Coalition seeks tariffs and duties on LSPTV products sold below market value and with the assistance of improper foreign government subsidies. Duties would be levied on any imports after Commerce’s preliminary determination beginning in November 2024. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_1386177487b1b45550719c3e`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- According to ImportGenius, the U.S. imported $709 million in fully assembled golf carts in 2024, with $703 million, or 99% of that, coming from China. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2025/04/30/tariffs-target-trumps-second-favorite-set-of-wheels-the-golf-cart.html), `claim_286a35d52415e6cfab3fe03d`, `chunk_9ccef08723eccd0bdd8ca04e`).
- CLUB CAR FIGHTS BACK AGAINST SURGING CHINESE IMPORTS AUGUSTA, Ga. (October 24, 2024) - Club Car®, as part of the American Personal Transportation Vehicle Manufacturers Coalition, filed critical circumstances allegations with the U.S. Department of Commerce on October 22, 2024, to address a massive increase in Chinese imports of low-speed personal transportation vehicles (LSPTVs). ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_4345836dc026fdeab150b533`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- On July 10, 2024, Commerce opened an AD/CVD investigation into Chinese LSPTV imports, and on August 2, 2024, the USITC unanimously confirmed that these imports are indeed harming U.S. manufacturers. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_c9a2cc22182f09699cac8104`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- The attempt by Chinese companies to dump low-quality products into the American market ahead of any duties by the United States has escalated so quickly that Club Car and the Coalition have called for urgent action to impose retroactive duties on these Chinese imports. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_e61749cebbf540586b6db3ff`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- The ITC has already found a reasonable indication that imports from China are materially injuring what is defined as the U.S. low-speed, personal transportation vehicle market. A final determination, which could include tariffs on Chinese-made golf carts, is scheduled to be announced on June 17. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2025/04/30/tariffs-target-trumps-second-favorite-set-of-wheels-the-golf-cart.html), `claim_ee2a13c68999cd33bac791b7`, `chunk_9ccef08723eccd0bdd8ca04e`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

### q_company_fc7c935e96c630b5d97e68a4_targeted: "Continental Automotive" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Chinese-made electric vehicles facing a combined tariff rate exceeding 100% due to Section 301 surcharges (which were unaffected by the SCOTUS ruling) layered on top of the 12.5% forced-labor tariff ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_4526706572161a7c9b1985e1`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- The Supreme Court struck down the 2025 IEEPA tariffs and a $166B refund pool is open. See your personalized refund opportunity & filing roadmap. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_570397308dafbe26196b4d61`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- Section 232 metals tariffs apply to auto components: after the April 6, 2026 restructure, articles wholly of steel/aluminum/copper carry 50% while derivative components substantially made of those metals carry 25% ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_58231e50145689061b90faf7`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- EU-built vehicles and auto parts fall under the EU-US trade deal's flat 15% all-inclusive rate, which applies in lieu of the 27.5% Section 232 auto regime. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_bdc846b20f84abb617080466`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- Section 122 base tariff expired at its 150-day limit on July 24, 2026 and was replaced the same day by the Section 301 forced-labor tariff ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_f18821c8a4008678ec65c963`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).

### q_company_8d42c7988edcfa40dca2f234_targeted: "Daimler Truck North America" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Companies assembling vehicles in the US or USMCA compliant trucks may qualify for partial tariff offsets. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_06db0f02f99555796b6a2f53`, `chunk_e649c4ed7e7f4cc81753092e`).
- The Trump administration has announced a 25% tariff on imported medium- and heavy-duty trucks , effective November 1, 2025, as part of a strategy to bolster U.S. manufacturing. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_4d4677d0e5f197daf60a30d7`, `chunk_3f33a3360e03fade7189ed9e`).
- Impose new 25% tariffs on medium and heavy-duty truck imports, effective November 1. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_6f0a6319ffe7a14a19a8e598`, `chunk_e649c4ed7e7f4cc81753092e`).
- Starting November 1, a 25% tariff will be applied to imported medium and heavy-duty trucks and parts, alongside a new 10% tariff on imported buses. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_716b574017275165f3b8b811`, `chunk_3f33a3360e03fade7189ed9e`).
- President Trump confirmed that a new 25% tariff on imported medium and heavy-duty trucks will go into effect November 1, impacting major exporters such as Mexico, Canada, and Japan. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_730540c7038c6f44bac0053f`, `chunk_e649c4ed7e7f4cc81753092e`).
- In just over a week, a 25% duty will take effect on imported medium and heavy-duty trucks. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_787191671b18ccad58a6c71f`, `chunk_e649c4ed7e7f4cc81753092e`).
- US President Donald Trump signed orders on Friday, October 17, 2025, to provide significant tariff relief for domestic auto and engine production while simultaneously imposing new duties on imported trucks and buses. The relief expands a credit for automakers equal to 3.75% of the suggested retail price for U.S.-assembled vehicles through 2030, which helps offset import tariffs on parts. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_85b950a6e7a031306da3095a`, `chunk_3f33a3360e03fade7189ed9e`).
- Well this has turned out badly for Daimler Truck North America and Volvo Group: they sided with the Trump administration in trying to shut down the electric truck market and now the administration is slapping them with 25% tariffs on heavy truck imports - slated to take effect today. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-markets-economics-activity-7312084937573036032-Cdbh), `claim_8c07446a22c86069f0a60e01`, `chunk_3f33a3360e03fade7189ed9e`).
- Truck makers such as Daimler Truck, Traton and Volvo are leveraging production in Mexico to qualify under USMCA rules and avoid the highest duties, including the 50% tariffs on steel and aluminium derivatives and components. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/supply-chain/tariff-analysis-deep-dive-the-most-important-changes-for-the-auto-industry/663875), `claim_a8bba49299d15cc9c3e47b5e`, `chunk_f3ccfe227af4fcf53abe2616`).
- US administration extended the scope of the 50% import tariffs for steel and aluminium goods, adding more than 400 additional items to the levy, including car parts such as chassis components, axles, steel wiring, brackets and mounts, and parts of heavy vehicles ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/supply-chain/tariff-analysis-deep-dive-the-most-important-changes-for-the-auto-industry/663875), `claim_ad1f6df19da5473995bac9ed`, `chunk_aadfedec0b4209946c7e44f7`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

### q_company_1ccd8d549b6d5b76fe835826_targeted: "Lear Corporation" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Country-specific tariffs (potentially above 10%) will follow on April 9, targeting countries with large U.S. trade deficits. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_0b0bf81bc30ed57f22a198c9`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The tariff is applied the moment a part enters the country, meaning repair shops and parts distributors are already absorbing the added costs. Some may seek to shift sourcing to domestic suppliers, but the U.S. currently lacks the manufacturing capacity to meet demand for many high-volume aftermarket components at scale or cost parity. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_4daa4b3cdd4365068fd3f226`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_ab080f1001bfac175d9d3696`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- According to an April 2 statement by the White House, the Trump Administration has issued a sweeping executive order that will impose a 10% universal tariff on nearly all imported goods beginning April 5. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_da67748cb0b190cab0585f4d`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Trump has imposed new 20% duties on Chinese imports, reinstated 25% tariffs on global steel and aluminum, and applied 25% tariffs on certain imports from Canada and Mexico that do not comply with a North American trade agreement over the U.S. fentanyl overdose crisis. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_e8a959b206e155f24d2177ff`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

### q_company_40f766afc78f2026929f2f25_targeted: "MAT Heavy Duty" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Now, a 50% tariff rate applies to stamped components such as bodyshop panels, brackets and structural elements, even if they would otherwise qualify as car parts, meaning the rate goes up from 27.5% (25% for USMCA compliance) to 50%. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/supply-chain/tariff-analysis-deep-dive-the-most-important-changes-for-the-auto-industry/663875), `claim_081a64a2fb5dd94a7a6dd1f9`, `chunk_aadfedec0b4209946c7e44f7`).
- Importers of automobiles under the United States-Mexico-Canada Agreement will be given the opportunity to certify their U.S. content and systems will be implemented such that the 25% tariff will only apply to the value of their non-U.S. content. ([source](https://www.whitehouse.gov/fact-sheets/2025/03/fact-sheet-president-donald-j-trump-adjusts-imports-of-automobiles-and-automobile-parts-into-the-united-states), `claim_0cc65990055af7a345fdfd22`, `chunk_a1de1eb7c3560e5de1d82ce3`).
- USMCA-compliant automobile parts will remain tariff-free until the Secretary of Commerce, in consultation with U.S. Customs and Border Protection (CBP), establishes a process to apply tariffs to their non-U.S. content. ([source](https://www.whitehouse.gov/fact-sheets/2025/03/fact-sheet-president-donald-j-trump-adjusts-imports-of-automobiles-and-automobile-parts-into-the-united-states), `claim_16c1c6547f390315ecf56eaf`, `chunk_a1de1eb7c3560e5de1d82ce3`).
- after the April 6, 2026 restructure, articles wholly of steel/aluminum/copper carry 50% while derivative components substantially made of those metals carry 25%, in each case assessed on the full customs value of the article ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_2f54b03de8ca32f7952beb1c`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- In August, the US administration extended the scope of the 50% import tariffs for steel and aluminium goods, adding more than 400 additional items to the levy, including car parts such as chassis components, axles, steel wiring, brackets and mounts, and parts of heavy vehicles. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/supply-chain/tariff-analysis-deep-dive-the-most-important-changes-for-the-auto-industry/663875), `claim_4fe3539febac090290f8f0de`, `chunk_aadfedec0b4209946c7e44f7`).
- EU-built vehicles and auto parts fall under the EU-US trade deal's flat 15% all-inclusive rate, which applies in lieu of the 27.5% Section 232 auto regime. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_5a70f66e557aec6b63765a01`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- The US and South Korea agreed on tariffs of 15% for vehicle imports and automotive parts imports in late July, replacing the previously threatened 25% rate. This came into effect on August 1, but as of early September, the agreement remains provisional and has not been formally finalised. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/supply-chain/tariff-analysis-deep-dive-the-most-important-changes-for-the-auto-industry/663875), `claim_683e83c74abc630bb890d3cd`, `chunk_aadfedec0b4209946c7e44f7`).
- The European Union reached a framework for a deal with the US at the end of July, which reduces the cost of shipping vehicles and parts from the EU to the US from a rate of 27.5% to 15%. Currently, this 15% tariff is not yet in place, with EU documents suggesting it will apply retroactively from August. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/supply-chain/tariff-analysis-deep-dive-the-most-important-changes-for-the-auto-industry/663875), `claim_6b679f47863b6b8dd6df98aa`, `chunk_aadfedec0b4209946c7e44f7`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

- A second wave of 25% tariffs on imported automotive parts is set to take effect Saturday. ([source](https://www.cbtnews.com/mercedes-to-build-new-vehicle-in-alabama-as-tariffs-reshape-auto-industry), `claim_b78b92d8bcd1315b1462c441`, `chunk_6a0cfe49c12d74073e9deb91`).

- Mercedes-Benz will be absorbing some of the impact of the tariffs to protect the price (MSRP) of its Model Year 2025 vehicles. ([source](https://www.mercedesofcalabasas.com/tariff-faqs.htm), `claim_18668f4d9419f91c4b6ca0ad`, `chunk_0683ba44a1ab5cb1a7046af8`).
- The vehicles in the Mercedes-Benz portfolio are affected by the tariffs at varying levels. ### q_company_88a2081314267701955f6546_targeted: "Nidec Elesys Americas Corp." automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Treat tariff permanence as a strategic baseline. The 25% Section 232 tariff on imported auto parts is not a temporary disruption. ([source](https://www.nextmsc.com/blogs/motor-vehicle-parts-market-tariffs-trade-wars-the-road-to-2030), `claim_2204d0ae7501a7b7766a8b87`, `chunk_d3805a59e94831ce6615252c`).
- The adjusted tariff rate cannot fall below 25%, and the benefit is limited to articles that are USMCA qualifying and that were melted and poured or smelted and cast in Mexico or Canada ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/05/automotive-tariffs-and-trade-remedy-investigations-navigating-shifts-in-the-us-trade-landscape), `claim_99b91bb3d56b4e28cb156452`, `chunk_fdbd0f793edcf9020372f316`).
- on April 23, the Department of Commerce opened a process to reduce applicable Section 232 tariffs on USMCA-qualifying steel and aluminum articles for companies that supply, directly or indirectly (through incorporation into parts), to US automobile or medium- and heavy-duty vehicle manufacturers ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/05/automotive-tariffs-and-trade-remedy-investigations-navigating-shifts-in-the-us-trade-landscape), `claim_f3eb7c58fca66a0a77642632`, `chunk_fdbd0f793edcf9020372f316`).

### q_company_8f5291e2a5a64e6a8f7f09e2_targeted: "Nile Automotive" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- President Trump has implemented a 25% tariff on foreign-made auto parts, including powertrain components, seats, and airbags, while introducing some key exemptions to reduce the strain on automakers. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_030ef6fab6c8784dfaff1fab`, `chunk_9f82b7462f46a2f16899791a`).
- The White House announced that passenger vehicles, certain trucks, and auto parts will be exempt from the new 10% global tariff set to take effect on February 24, 2026. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_5660f160c83adcce52497d18`, `chunk_e8a1ffd62f7b512ed56a6a50`).
- The Supreme Court struck down the 2025 IEEPA tariffs and a $166B refund pool is open. See your personalized refund opportunity & filing roadmap. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_570397308dafbe26196b4d61`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- Ford Motor has raised prices on three Mexico-produced models—the Mustang Mach-E, Maverick, and Bronco Sport—by up to $2,000, citing mid-year pricing adjustments and the impact of U.S. tariffs imposed by President Trump. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_6dfd3abc11136b304bdc1102`, `chunk_9f82b7462f46a2f16899791a`).
- EU-built vehicles and auto parts fall under the EU-US trade deal's flat 15% all-inclusive rate, which applies in lieu of the 27.5% Section 232 auto regime. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_8fbf1da80f96e1c2a94540d4`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- China remains the most heavily penalized, with Chinese-made electric vehicles facing a combined tariff rate exceeding 100% due to Section 301 surcharges (which were unaffected by the SCOTUS ruling) layered on top of the 12.5% forced-labor tariff ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_93936735327eed9a8a5c8538`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).

### q_company_a51a3f8338d12ca01a577774_targeted: "NVH Korea" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- President Donald Trump announced he will be increasing tariffs on South Korean goods from 15% to 25%, citing South Korea’s failure to enact a trade agreement signed in July 2025. The tariffs will affect a wide range of goods, including automobiles, lumber, and pharmaceuticals ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_1eac734959b2c600fe256d7a`, `chunk_f3acc3ddb27ff46874313455`).
- Vehicles from the U.K. are now subject to a 10% tariff, and, in the past few weeks, 15% automobile import tariffs were established with several other major trading partners, including Japan, South Korea and the EU. ([source](https://www.coxautoinc.com/insights-hub/the-trump-tariff-stance-has-shifted-where-are-we-now), `claim_7e47373680b365080bbecda3`, `chunk_c70d0065a7c97ff634980ede`).
- Deals the Trump administration reached with the EU, Japan and South Korea in 2025 reduced U.S. auto tariffs on vehicles and parts from those countries to 15 percent, from 25 percent, while a deal with the United Kingdom lowered tariffs on most imports to 10 percent. ([source](https://www.facebook.com/AutoNews/posts/automakers-and-suppliers-hope-the-15-percent-tariff-on-vehicle-and-parts-imports/1598119755507085), `claim_c83be9ec47c077161de5197f`, `chunk_7aa2f53664e70dec8b6cffbc`).
- The United States and South Korea have reached a significant trade agreement, reducing U.S. tariffs on South Korean automobile exports from 25% to 15%. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_ce0ecd21755859752b8e5274`, `chunk_4dd34d22b4ec1ee472f18fb9`).
- President Donald Trump has announced a 25 percent tariff on goods imported from Japan and South Korea, alongside new import taxes on 12 other nations, set to take effect on August 1. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_f33873bd7ae1cd3062227e17`, `chunk_e64fc3d84b6efb93481b3a67`).
- According to general estimates from Cox Automotive, the total bill so far in 2025 for vehicles imported from Korea would be $3.8 billion, or roughly $4,000 per unit (if tariffs were in place since Jan. 1). ([source](https://www.coxautoinc.com/insights-hub/the-trump-tariff-stance-has-shifted-where-are-we-now), `claim_946b50c548f7f1ce83cd3ccb`, `chunk_c70d0065a7c97ff634980ede`).

- To protect themselves from tariff exposure, importers are diversifying toward countries like Brazil, Mexico, Malaysia, and India. ([source](https://www.knowledge-sourcing.com/resources/thought-articles/how-tariffs-are-affecting-the-u-s-automotive-tire-import-market), `claim_1ae2e2516909923caf0dcd71`, `chunk_ef624bd18e0d729a99579de8`).
- The following factors are influencing the U.S. automotive tire import market: - Importers were forced to move their sourcing to Southeast Asia due to tariffs on Chinese tires. ([source](https://www.knowledge-sourcing.com/resources/thought-articles/how-tariffs-are-affecting-the-u-s-automotive-tire-import-market), `claim_2d8a17fa8c8c1e1205698ca0`, `chunk_ef624bd18e0d729a99579de8`).
- After the Chinese tariffs, Thailand, which had been the largest supplier of passenger and commercial truck tires, got hit in 2024 with new antidumping tariffs on truck and bus radial (TBR) tires. ([source](https://www.knowledge-sourcing.com/resources/thought-articles/how-tariffs-are-affecting-the-u-s-automotive-tire-import-market), `claim_4c40403383fc564565eeddff`, `chunk_ef624bd18e0d729a99579de8`).

- As of March 26, 2025, the U.S. has implemented an additional 25% tariff on imported vehicles and parts, bringing the total tariff rate to 27.5%. - Manufacturer response Porsche has not announced any official response to the tariffs. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_50aa324ec29ae46cc4a08a86`, `chunk_574337eb0dddbbe08222ab07`).
- Pricing impact Porsche confirmed a 1.2%–2.9% price increase for the new year. Orders delivered before January 5th are exempt from the adjustment. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_9004a48e6122ab4458b3fe91`, `chunk_574337eb0dddbbe08222ab07`).
- Trump's 25% tariff on all foreign-made cars is a big blow even for luxury carmakers like Porsche. ([source](https://www.facebook.com/deutschewellenews/videos/will-trump-tariffs-force-luxury-carmakers-to-move-production-to-the-us/1376667536802890), `claim_d712332800bb305acd11dd9e`, `chunk_3bff9f2a3575a5aefad456a8`).

### q_company_d52e9e16bbe521b4e9847297_targeted: "PPG Industries Inc." automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6ff61cf9e3aaed6db9d9a91b`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The 25% tariff will be applied to imported passenger vehicles (sedans, SUVs, crossovers, minivans, cargo vans) and light trucks, as well as key automobile parts (engines, transmissions, powertrain parts, and electrical components) ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_7ebba2803b298e44664f6fcb`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The tariff is applied the moment a part enters the country, meaning repair shops and parts distributors are already absorbing the added costs. Some may seek to shift sourcing to domestic suppliers, but the U.S. currently lacks the manufacturing capacity to meet demand for many high-volume aftermarket components at scale or cost parity. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_a77e4ee5e7c2a9e53661bd13`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- The 10% universal tariff that starts on April 5 will apply to nearly all imported goods, including vehicles and vehicle components. This serves as a baseline rate across countries. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f6d160242ac95d453ec775f5`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Deals the Trump administration reached with the EU, Japan and South Korea in 2025 reduced U.S. auto tariffs on vehicles and parts from those countries to 15 percent, from 25 percent, while a deal with the United Kingdom lowered tariffs on most imports to 10 percent. ([source](https://www.facebook.com/AutoNews/posts/automakers-and-suppliers-hope-the-15-percent-tariff-on-vehicle-and-parts-imports/1598119755507085), `claim_c83be9ec47c077161de5197f`, `chunk_7aa2f53664e70dec8b6cffbc`).
- Automakers and suppliers hope the 15 percent tariff on vehicle and parts imports from the European Union, Japan and South Korea will serve as a baseline for upcoming North American trade negotiations with Canada and Mexico. ([source](https://www.facebook.com/AutoNews/posts/automakers-and-suppliers-hope-the-15-percent-tariff-on-vehicle-and-parts-imports/1598119755507085), `claim_d3c18c91e658f29addf52219`, `chunk_7aa2f53664e70dec8b6cffbc`).
- The effort is positioned as “part of a broader effort to rebalance U.S. trade deficits and bolster domestic manufacturing.” The action, issued under the International Emergency Economic Powers Act (IEEPA), marks a significant escalation in the administration’s trade policy ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_a874fa03ce15b6db3caef86e`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Country-specific tariffs (potentially above 10%) will follow on April 9, targeting countries with large U.S. trade deficits. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_0b0bf81bc30ed57f22a198c9`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Automakers such as Ford, Nissan, and Mitsubishi have warned of potential vehicle price increases. Ford has stated it may raise prices on vehicles built in May. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_e0d866a1b68312f57acb116f`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).

### q_company_c89f5b7a127c7ebb39ac7795_targeted: "Seoyon E-HWA" automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- The 10% Section 122 base tariff expired at its 150-day limit on July 24, 2026 and was replaced the same day by the Section 301 forced-labor tariff — 10% or 12.5% depending on the economy ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_39b31efd3f593a110df702d1`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- EU-origin goods instead pay a 15% all-inclusive ceiling under the EU-US deal effective July 1, 2026; Section 301 and Section 232 surcharges raise the effective rate further on covered goods ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_7da457f8d020529ad3ed80bd`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- China remains the most heavily penalized, with Chinese-made electric vehicles facing a combined tariff rate exceeding 100% due to Section 301 surcharges (which were unaffected by the SCOTUS ruling) layered on top of the 12.5% forced-labor tariff ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_9107d4c0c6732d5cfb8de49a`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- The base layer is the 2.5% MFN rate plus the Section 301 forced-labor tariff (10% or 12.5% by tier) for most countries — EU-origin goods instead pay a 15% all-inclusive ceiling under the EU-US deal effective July 1, 2026 ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_c913340cdf9409275ed2aca5`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

### q_company_897a9db8aebed83bd2799b36_targeted: "Southern Switches Corp." automotive vehicles auto parts tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_087b5f3d77779421ee3026cb`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6ff61cf9e3aaed6db9d9a91b`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The 25% tariff will be applied to imported passenger vehicles (sedans, SUVs, crossovers, minivans, cargo vans) and light trucks, as well as key automobile parts (engines, transmissions, powertrain parts, and electrical components), with processes to expand tariffs on additional parts “if necessary.” ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_41c16ac91af5a877c4949d08`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Section 232 tariffs impose a 25% duty on imported automobiles and certain automobile parts, authorized under Section 232 of the Trade Expansion Act of 1962 based on a finding that automotive imports threaten U.S. national security. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_fff4e9e6793048131e2be8bd`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- The tariff on finished vehicles took effect April 3, 2025, with auto parts tariffs following on May 3, 2025. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_d52d31c5a0fa1530090ae4fc`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- The 10% universal tariff that starts on April 5 will apply to nearly all imported goods, including vehicles and vehicle components. This serves as a baseline rate across countries. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_8f6d22ad2aee4dbd6e121c22`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The 10% universal tariff that starts on April 5 will apply to nearly all imported goods, including vehicles and vehicle components. This serves as a baseline rate across countries. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f054b206351102eee66de568`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Section 232 tariffs apply to automobiles regardless of drivetrain type. Electric vehicles imported from countries without bilateral agreements face the full 25% tariff. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_7cf17ecfa4a11e4efabda341`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- USMCA-qualifying EVs from Canada and Mexico may be exempt, subject to rules of origin compliance. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_82d2ce04ae1c83afc05fe7ac`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- White & Case, "Trump administration posts proposed automotive parts Section 232 tariff expansion" Data cited: January 2026 inclusions round, ITA review process, October 2025 pending decisions Source: White & Case Published: January 2026 ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_8a2c17d54b1124ddb1a83ab6`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- Federal Register, "Notice of the Opening of the Inclusions Window for the Section 232 Automobile Parts Tariff Inclusions Process" Data cited: April 2026 window dates, submission process, docket number Source: Federal Register Published: March 24, 2026 ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_d33da58ca3c47b73da7a4472`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- Commercial vehicles including sedans, SUVs, crossover utility vehicles, minivans, cargo vans, and light trucks are subject to 25% Section 232 duties under HTS heading 9903.94.01. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_dd62b0bc751af23dad5dab42`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- The offset works as follows: - Year 1 (April 3, 2025 to April 30, 2026): Eligible manufacturers can apply for an offset equal to 3.75% of the aggregate Manufacturer's Suggested Retail Price (MSRP) of all U.S.-assembled vehicles ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_85b75272e87e601c1a2d8cb7`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- U.S. Department of Commerce, "New Auto Tariff Offset Process" Data cited: Offset rates (3.75%/2.5%), documentation requirements, timeline Source: Commerce Department Published: 2025 ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_a72b00173d7acf9d8ea98004`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- March 13: One-Month Exemption for Automakers, Steel and Aluminum Tariffs in Place Last week, President Trump granted a one-month exemption on recent tariffs on imports from Mexico and Canada for U.S. automakers. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_eb99eb7d816b88c3853bd978`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- Trump said in February that he intended to impose auto tariffs but later agreed to delay some auto tariffs after a push by the three largest U.S. automakers for a waiver. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f28b2f29c3240c99d9f8236b`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).

([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_087b5f3d77779421ee3026cb`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The 25% tariff will be applied to imported passenger vehicles (sedans, SUVs, crossovers, minivans, cargo vans) and light trucks, as well as key automobile parts (engines, transmissions, powertrain parts, and electrical components), with processes to expand tariffs on additional parts “if necessary.” ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_41c16ac91af5a877c4949d08`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- On February 18, President Trump also announced plans to impose a new tariff on automotive imports, tentatively scheduled to go into effect on April 2. The tariffs will “be in the neighborhood of 25%” and may include another tariff on semiconductors (used extensively in automotive production). ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_57840d97414e2334eda8fedc`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6ff61cf9e3aaed6db9d9a91b`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The 25% tariff will be applied to imported passenger vehicles (sedans, SUVs, crossovers, minivans, cargo vans) and light trucks, as well as key automobile parts (engines, transmissions, powertrain parts, and electrical components), with processes to expand tariffs on additional parts “if necessary.” ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_72cc87dbaafaf8ac196d5089`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- A new 25% tariff on imported auto parts officially took effect at 12:01 a.m. EDT on May 3. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_bb6c67d7275f7246968718b7`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- on April 23, the Department of Commerce opened a process to reduce applicable Section 232 tariffs on USMCA-qualifying steel and aluminum articles for companies that supply, directly or indirectly (through incorporation into parts), to US automobile or medium- and heavy-duty vehicle manufacturers ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/05/automotive-tariffs-and-trade-remedy-investigations-navigating-shifts-in-the-us-trade-landscape), `claim_f3eb7c58fca66a0a77642632`, `chunk_fdbd0f793edcf9020372f316`).
- The 10% universal tariff that starts on April 5 will apply to nearly all imported goods, including vehicles and vehicle components. This serves as a baseline rate across countries. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f6d160242ac95d453ec775f5`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Safety-critical components like airbags, mirrors, seats, and batteries will be subject to the same 25% tariff starting May 3. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_ff49036f0d5592cec2865058`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).

- According to S&P Global, the potential for a prolonged 25% tariff on imports from Mexico and Canada, along with recently announced updates to tariffs on steel and aluminum, could have a multi-billion-dollar impact on Ford’s and GM’s profitability metrics. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_4857a771a46959b70807de96`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- Treat tariff permanence as a strategic baseline. The 25% Section 232 tariff on imported auto parts is not a temporary disruption. ([source](https://www.nextmsc.com/blogs/motor-vehicle-parts-market-tariffs-trade-wars-the-road-to-2030), `claim_c467452b8b2a8f12bfb504e8`, `chunk_d3805a59e94831ce6615252c`).
- The 25% tariffs on imported vehicles and parts, effective since April 2025, have already driven a 2.5% surge in average new vehicle prices in April, reaching $48,699 ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_de2c3dcdfa2a94c66c4aec97`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- Trump has imposed new 20% duties on Chinese imports, reinstated 25% tariffs on global steel and aluminum, and applied 25% tariffs on certain imports from Canada and Mexico that do not comply with a North American trade agreement over the U.S. fentanyl overdose crisis. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_e8a959b206e155f24d2177ff`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- Trump said in February that he intended to impose auto tariffs but later agreed to delay some auto tariffs after a push by the three largest U.S. automakers for a waiver. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f28b2f29c3240c99d9f8236b`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- The adjusted tariff rate cannot fall below 25%, and the benefit is limited to articles that are USMCA qualifying and that were melted and poured or smelted and cast in Mexico or Canada ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/05/automotive-tariffs-and-trade-remedy-investigations-navigating-shifts-in-the-us-trade-landscape), `claim_fc6d4e59cc843944f5d964f3`, `chunk_fdbd0f793edcf9020372f316`).
- March 13: One-Month Exemption for Automakers, Steel and Aluminum Tariffs in Place Last week, President Trump granted a one-month exemption on recent tariffs on imports from Mexico and Canada for U.S. automakers. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_fcdeaa9a3a4c38c683eaa979`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).

- Toyota Motor told CNBC in an email that automakers are not affected by the tariffs imposed under the International Emergency Economic Powers Act, or IEEPA, which the court struck down Friday. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2026/02/24/automakers-tariffs-prices.html), `claim_2ddf17a9c5c86e709b0de90f`, `chunk_4bd342fb0c43e6d783de11f2`).

- Toyota, the world's largest automaker by volume, saw net income fall 25% in the first nine months of its fiscal year 2026, according to a filing. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2026/02/24/automakers-tariffs-prices.html), `claim_7901070510c92ae8b5f9e926`, `chunk_4bd342fb0c43e6d783de11f2`).
- Toyota told CNBC in an email that the tariffs are "highly disruptive" and "cannot be sustained." - Toyota appears poised to receive the biggest tariff bill of any automaker, projecting ¥1.45 trillion ($9.1 billion) in costs in its 2026 fiscal year, which ends March 31. - The Trump administration has doubled tariffs on steel and aluminum imports to 50 percent, citing national security and efforts to bolster domestic production. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_f5cbf389fb13c8d25536fee7`, `chunk_e64fc3d84b6efb93481b3a67`).

- Volvo is removing the S90 sedan from its U.S. lineup due to the new 125 percent tariff on China-made vehicles. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_04528098c3a3e490e2c806be`, `chunk_e5de37b25e04e36e86e3d5a7`).
- The company plans to focus more heavily on its top-selling SUVs, and is also considering expanding production at its South Carolina plant to offset future tariff impacts. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_11d9bb7e8b2359d26cb7869d`, `chunk_e5de37b25e04e36e86e3d5a7`).
- Volvo is evaluating plans to scale up production at its South Carolina facility to strengthen its U.S. manufacturing footprint. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_186a4ac92e9af7a08b3935a5`, `chunk_e5de37b25e04e36e86e3d5a7`).
- Volvo's EV, the EX30, has begun production at its Belgian factory, to avoid the steeper Chinese tariffs, where the EX30 was previously being manufactured. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_49885cacd3d3242a31c3dc0e`, `chunk_e5de37b25e04e36e86e3d5a7`).
- he EX90, which, despite being assembled in the U.S., relies heavily on European components subject to import duties. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_8b590ef321651687e9b58e74`, `chunk_e64fc3d84b6efb93481b3a67`).
- Volvo is moving production of the XC60 SUV from Sweden to its plant in Ridgeville, South Carolina. ([source](https://www.truecar.com/auto-tariffs-explained), `claim_92fcb885a43df7baa98f0fa2`, `chunk_e5de37b25e04e36e86e3d5a7`).
- Volvo Cars has announced plans to cut 3,000 jobs globally, primarily impacting office-based positions in Sweden, as part of an 18 billion Swedish kronor ($1.89 billion) cost-cutting initiative. The layoffs include eliminating 1,000 consultant roles and reducing investments worldwide. This move reflects the company’s effort to address increasing challenges in the global market, such as tariff pressures. Recent geopolitical tensions, including potential U.S.-EU tariffs, have further destabilized the sector, prompting Volvo to withdraw its financial guidance for 2025 and 2026. ([source](https://digitaldealer.com/news/us-tariff-tracker-impact-automaker-response/164521), `claim_f3d4c503bb146c3519405ec1`, `chunk_9f82b7462f46a2f16899791a`).

### q_policyquery_7f5dfd37b0571aba57f95b92: automobiles auto parts Section 232 effective date status 2026

- Auto parts for passenger vehicles and light trucks, as defined by Executive Order 14257, issued April 2, 2025, and subject to Section 232 duties as of May 3, 2025, are not subject to the reciprocal duties effective April 5, 2025, pursuant to heading 9903.01.33, HTSUS. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_09ebb6e8151b2f7bd451f4c2`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Heading 9903.01.33, HTSUS, exempting products subject to Section 232 from the reciprocal tariffs, applies to auto parts for passenger vehicles and light trucks. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_209c51c13c4027cfd6a30271`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Light trucks classified in subheadings 8704.21.01, 8704.31.01, 8704.41.00, 8704.51.00, and 8704.60.00 are subject to the duties. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_65ed1b7a9f8e263b9f754e6d`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Commercial vehicles, such as sedans, sport utility vehicles, crossover utility vehicles, minivans, cargo vans and light trucks, provided for in heading 9903.94.01, HTSUS, are subject to the Section 232 duties pursuant to the Proclamation. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_f5a33b50594a512943003b0d`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).

Limitations: current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

### q_policyquery_674da3345b06fe290ac2e055: automobiles auto parts Section 232 country of origin exemptions exclusions

- On March 26, President Trump issued Proclamation 10908, “Adjusting Imports of Automobiles and Automobile Parts Into the United States” (Proclamation), imposing a 25% additional tariff on imports of passenger vehicles, light trucks, and specific automotive parts under Section 232 of the Trade Expansion Act of 1962 (Section 232). ([source](https://www.troutman.com/insights/new-section-232-tariffs-on-automobiles-and-automotive-parts), `claim_2564a2f5d7a08d47fcddb014`, `chunk_1f9bb15f2a5a55b2d2c10d4b`).
- President Trump has used his authority under Section 232 of the Trade Expansion Act of 1962 — the same authority used to impose tariffs on steel, aluminum, and copper — to impose a 25% tariff on automobiles and automobile parts. The action is grounded in a national security investigation originally initiated during the first Trump administration. The proclamation was issued March 26, 2025. ([source](https://greatlakescustomslaw.com/automobile-and-auto-parts-tariffs-hts-list-section-232), `claim_64e5dc71e079e997c3a0f113`, `chunk_924aa0196caf66670103f14d`).
- The tariff on specified automotive parts will take effect on a date to be announced in the Federal Register, but no later than May 3, 2025. ([source](https://www.troutman.com/insights/new-section-232-tariffs-on-automobiles-and-automotive-parts), `claim_b5d1ffdd61884add5a745da4`, `chunk_1f9bb15f2a5a55b2d2c10d4b`).
- Several new Chapter 99 subheadings were established to administer the tariff: - 9903.94.01 — Covered passenger vehicles and light trucks subject to 25% tariff ([source](https://greatlakescustomslaw.com/automobile-and-auto-parts-tariffs-hts-list-section-232), `claim_b5d7b1460415456ce78a432c`, `chunk_924aa0196caf66670103f14d`).
- Commercial vehicles, such as sedans, sport utility vehicles, crossover utility vehicles, minivans, cargo vans and light trucks, provided for in heading 9903.94.01, HTSUS, are subject to the Section 232 duties pursuant to the Proclamation. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_9a0b1489aeb7b50a6afb97ff`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Yes, used passenger vehicles and trucks are subject to the Section 232 duties pursuant to the Proclamation, except those manufactured in a year at least 25 years prior to the year of the date of entry. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_a755309dbf7188d6a2e597a1`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Automotive parts imported under the U.S.-Mexico-Canada Agreement (USMCA) are temporarily excluded from these tariffs. This exclusion will transition to a partial exemption based on U.S. content in the future, once the U.S. Secretary of Commerce establishes a process to calculate and apply the tariff solely to the non-U.S. content of each part and issues a corresponding notice in the Federal Register. ([source](https://www.troutman.com/insights/new-section-232-tariffs-on-automobiles-and-automotive-parts), `claim_bde15b6b6509a981c298f117`, `chunk_1f9bb15f2a5a55b2d2c10d4b`).
- For USMCA-eligible automobile parts, the exemption is broader: USMCA-certified parts are fully exempt from the Section 232 tariff (not just the non-U.S. content portion), provided they are self-certified by the importer as intended for automobile production or repair in the United States. ([source](https://greatlakescustomslaw.com/automobile-and-auto-parts-tariffs-hts-list-section-232), `claim_d825f56197d89cdad6d3436e`, `chunk_924aa0196caf66670103f14d`).
- Yes, parts of automobiles that qualify for preferential tariff treatment under USMCA, self-certified by importers to be used for automobile production or repair activity in the United States, are eligible to use HTS 9903.94.06. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_99d8d019e5c207e71bef09e0`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Duty drawback will not be available for the 25% tariff imposed pursuant to the Proclamation. ([source](https://www.troutman.com/insights/new-section-232-tariffs-on-automobiles-and-automotive-parts), `claim_b724a2ac23ca923831199f62`, `chunk_1f9bb15f2a5a55b2d2c10d4b`).
- Duty drawback is not available for automobiles, light trucks, or automobile parts covered by Presidential Proclamation 10908. ([source](https://greatlakescustomslaw.com/automobile-and-auto-parts-tariffs-hts-list-section-232), `claim_c95205060b3865d49cebc16b`, `chunk_924aa0196caf66670103f14d`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

Conflicting claim groups: [["claim_bde15b6b6509a981c298f117", "claim_d825f56197d89cdad6d3436e"], ["claim_99d8d019e5c207e71bef09e0", "claim_bde15b6b6509a981c298f117"]]

### q_policyquery_a58aababb56181982268ca18: automobiles auto parts Section 232 duty stacking valuation rates

- Section 232 auto tariffs interact with USMCA preferences, bilateral deals, stacking rules, and a shifting inclusions list. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_031cb4b6255fe06107da0e80`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- Commercial vehicles, such as sedans, sport utility vehicles, crossover utility vehicles, minivans, cargo vans and light trucks, provided for in heading 9903.94.01, HTSUS, are subject to the Section 232 duties pursuant to the Proclamation. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_30344721e74e9ff976f00588`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- However, parts eligible under the U.S.-Mexico-Canada Agreement (other than automobile knock-down kits of parts compilations) are exempt from the tariff until the Department of Commerce establishes a process to apply the tariff only to non-U.S. content. ([source](https://www.strtrade.com/trade-news-resources/str-trade-report/trade-report/june/offsets-available-for-section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_313ec476d188a96051d3594f`, `chunk_2afa4d0956c7101781c00077`).
- Yes, parts of automobiles that qualify for preferential tariff treatment under USMCA, self-certified by importers to be used for automobile production or repair activity in the United States, are eligible to use HTS 9903.94.06. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_366e41899d3f5f520bd45b68`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- President Trump issued last month a proclamation providing that, for automobiles assembled in the U.S., manufacturers will be eligible to apply for offsets to the Section 232 tariff in the amount of (1) 3.75 percent of the aggregate manufacturer’s suggested retail price value of all automobiles they assemble in the U.S. from April 3, 2025, through April 30, 2026 ([source](https://www.strtrade.com/trade-news-resources/str-trade-report/trade-report/june/offsets-available-for-section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_4f99c1db83b015e806eea989`, `chunk_2afa4d0956c7101781c00077`).
- Section 232 auto tariffs do not stack with: - Other Section 232 tariffs (e.g., steel/aluminum) - Section 122 tariffs ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_506a8b99be6f1e230845d5a6`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- Yes. Section 232 tariffs apply to automobiles regardless of drivetrain type. Electric vehicles imported from countries without bilateral agreements face the full 25% tariff. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_5f444a0403b0f887d5a14fec`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- USMCA-qualifying EVs from Canada and Mexico may be exempt, subject to rules of origin compliance. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_82d2ce04ae1c83afc05fe7ac`, `chunk_35f9302ea50b3e44bbd8560b`).
- Heading 9903.01.33, HTSUS, exempting products subject to Section 232 from the reciprocal tariffs, applies to auto parts for passenger vehicles and light trucks. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_8d5e1017e9c7e2aa26786975`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- A 25 percent Section 232 tariff took effect May 3 for auto parts, which include engines and engine parts, transmissions and powertrain parts, electrical components, and parts of passenger vehicles and light trucks classified under specified HTSUS subheadings. ([source](https://www.strtrade.com/trade-news-resources/str-trade-report/trade-report/june/offsets-available-for-section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_aafde67173e6de978eac5651`, `chunk_2afa4d0956c7101781c00077`).
- Yes, used passenger vehicles and trucks are subject to the Section 232 duties pursuant to the Proclamation, except those manufactured in a year at least 25 years prior to the year of the date of entry. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_ba658f9c331832b91cabc6b6`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- Commercial vehicles including sedans, SUVs, crossover utility vehicles, minivans, cargo vans, and light trucks are subject to 25% Section 232 duties under HTS heading 9903.94.01. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_c4053968587e34ce64815eec`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- Only auto parts for passenger vehicles and light trucks, as defined by Executive Order 14257, issued April 2, 2025, and subject to Section 232 duties as of May 3, 2025, are not subject to the reciprocal duties effective April 5, 2025, pursuant to heading 9903.01.33, HTSUS. ([source](https://www.cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-232-additional-faqs-autos), `claim_cd36f1db39d40b7fd62348f9`, `chunk_fe5e2939202360977b32001f`).
- The Department of Commerce has established recurring two-week windows each quarter (January, April, July, and October) during which domestic manufacturers can submit requests to add new auto parts to the list of products subject to the 25% Section 232 tariff. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_f3ef424f06d5356a3242b19b`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- Auto parts that qualify for preferential treatment under USMCA may be eligible for HTS 9903.94.06, which can reduce or eliminate Section 232 duties. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-auto-parts-tariffs), `claim_f7d98cbdd1f69bcb24d84444`, `chunk_9b9e42c4f3a83c807c83fa72`).
- Section 232 tariffs impose a 25% duty on imported automobiles and certain automobile parts, authorized under Section 232 of the Trade Expansion Act of 1962 based on a finding that automotive imports threaten U.S. national security. - The effort is positioned as “part of a broader effort to rebalance U.S. trade deficits and bolster domestic manufacturing.” The action, issued under the International Emergency Economic Powers Act (IEEPA), marks a significant escalation in the administration’s trade policy ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_3bfad36c2d92d0f18504060a`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Toyota and Stellantis rely on Canadian-made vehicles for 18% and 14% of their U.S. sales, respectively, while only 5% of Ford’s U.S. sales came from Canadian plants. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_48d89ff949a3957b7f2f3d80`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- Major automakers like Volkswagen, Stellantis, Nissan, Mazda, Honda, and Ford rely heavily on Mexican production and exports. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_4c6cdd6db29004da5eba6ae3`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- The policy throws a wrench into the interconnected auto supply chain that was established decades ago in North America, a system that prioritizes efficiency and specialization over international boundaries. ([source](https://www.ajc.com/news/business/georgias-auto-plants-wont-protect-from-tariff-price-hikes-experts-say/NNSAAH2QXNEGRHG5NU4SGTQLWE), `claim_594ba95dbaa60beed1101801`, `chunk_521e7a529c03da5eac682837`).
- Vehicles imported from Canada or Mexico that do not meet USMCA compliance standards will also be subject to up to 25% tariffs. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_62f8c3f547a5afe3266dc01f`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Stellantis halted production at its Windsor Assembly Plant in Canada for two weeks and paused production at its Toluca Assembly Plant in Mexico for the month of April. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6656c396037eef7027f21c89`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- The administration indicates that tariffs may remain indefinitely, until trade balances are “resolved.” ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6a461d27687c7ce8b6ea01b3`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6ff61cf9e3aaed6db9d9a91b`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- President Donald Trump announced steep tariffs on automobile imports Wednesday, an economic punishment intended to push more cars down American assembly lines. ([source](https://www.ajc.com/news/business/georgias-auto-plants-wont-protect-from-tariff-price-hikes-experts-say/NNSAAH2QXNEGRHG5NU4SGTQLWE), `claim_70652ebb273b747376e3e013`, `chunk_521e7a529c03da5eac682837`).
- It thrusts consumers in Georgia and across the country onto the front lines of a potential trade war, asking car buyers to endure higher prices for the potential of more factories on American soil, which could take years to be built. ([source](https://www.ajc.com/news/business/georgias-auto-plants-wont-protect-from-tariff-price-hikes-experts-say/NNSAAH2QXNEGRHG5NU4SGTQLWE), `claim_8550f5b0d9d7fad67f66df66`, `chunk_521e7a529c03da5eac682837`).
- GM has paused production of its BrightDrop electric vans in Ontario until October 2025 to retool in preparation for the 2026 BrightDrop model. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_b443a23dcd2e6a8673b6c3b7`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- Trump said in February that he intended to impose auto tariffs but later agreed to delay some auto tariffs after a push by the three largest U.S. automakers for a waiver. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_cead51a8024130eee9a15382`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- Nissan is ramping up production at underutilized U.S. plants, while Honda announced a plan to relocate some hybrid production from Japan to Indiana. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_db896418c436f3d46acec858`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- The 25% tariffs on imported vehicles and parts, effective since April 2025, have already driven a 2.5% surge in average new vehicle prices in April, reaching $48,699 ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_de2c3dcdfa2a94c66c4aec97`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- Ford, General Motors, and Stellantis, on March 5. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_e6281f0ff51884cae07eecb8`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- The additional country- and product-specific tariffs that go into effect April 9 concern vehicles and parts from countries with large trade deficits with the U.S. —such as Germany, Japan, South Korea, and China. These rates may rise to 25% or higher. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f8383b155b5d37e37fa6eff1`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- Automakers such as Ford, Nissan, and Mitsubishi have warned of potential vehicle price increases. Ford has stated it may raise prices on vehicles built in May. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f9a9bb9a9ae32c096b354f45`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).

### q_company_105d9963b102a6ae236572eb_discovery: "Anovion Technologies" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- A separate anti-subsidy investigation into Chinese anode-grade graphite by the Commerce Department on May 20th resulted in a preliminary countervailing duty of 6.55% for most producers. ([source](https://christopherchico.substack.com/p/why-your-next-ev-battery-will-cost), `claim_10f7a3bafe79a896a4144650`, `chunk_96c2a3cecff1407f2bceba30`).
- The most recent influencing factors include the One Big Beautiful Bill Act, as well as tariffs. Donnelly stated that Anovion is still assessing the impacts of the OBBBA ([source](https://thepostsearchlight.com/2025/07/16/anovion-still-aiming-for-2026-construction), `claim_23c36e9e66d2d4e1c2523e44`, `chunk_dab5e9bf52eac3bf75a16221`).
- Anovion wants to get into business at scale in Bainbridge as fast as we prudently can to address both the national security needs of the US and build out a critical part of the battery supply chain ([source](https://thepostsearchlight.com/2025/07/16/anovion-still-aiming-for-2026-construction), `claim_274aa3eb08f6be7b58e5245f`, `chunk_dab5e9bf52eac3bf75a16221`).
- The petitioner in both the anti-dumping and anti-subsidy cases is the American Active Anode Material Producers, an ad hoc coalition of U.S. producers. It includes Anovion Technologies of Sanborn, New York ([source](https://christopherchico.substack.com/p/why-your-next-ev-battery-will-cost), `claim_43b3ab73fce5f9fbc2c5f0c6`, `chunk_96c2a3cecff1407f2bceba30`).
- Anovion Technologies officially broke ground for its new Bainbridge facility. Since then, there has been little visible construction at the industrial park site ([source](https://thepostsearchlight.com/2025/07/16/anovion-still-aiming-for-2026-construction), `claim_66ce8cc2c92735a07f8e76d4`, `chunk_dab5e9bf52eac3bf75a16221`).
- In July 2025, the United States issued a preliminary anti-dumping duty of 93.5% on active anode material from China. ([source](https://christopherchico.substack.com/p/why-your-next-ev-battery-will-cost), `claim_7e549581151ef6b5960daa2b`, `chunk_96c2a3cecff1407f2bceba30`).
- The current market for graphite anode material China completely dominates the global anode supply chain. The numbers are staggering. ([source](https://christopherchico.substack.com/p/why-your-next-ev-battery-will-cost), `claim_88027875896bf377582db3b6`, `chunk_96c2a3cecff1407f2bceba30`).
- Anovion was also announced as being in talks with the Export-Import Bank of the United States for $400 million in project financing. Donnelly stated that they are still working with the Ex-Im Bank to meet the criteria ([source](https://thepostsearchlight.com/2025/07/16/anovion-still-aiming-for-2026-construction), `claim_adb2e4fec45255c1cd569a30`, `chunk_dab5e9bf52eac3bf75a16221`).
- Anovion: The company operates a small facility of up to 5,000 tpa capacity today and is building toward a 40,000 tpa synthetic plant in Georgia later this decade with federal grant support. ([source](https://christopherchico.substack.com/p/why-your-next-ev-battery-will-cost), `claim_d8d7a87635e9d6aa01cc0331`, `chunk_96c2a3cecff1407f2bceba30`).
- As 95%+ of synthetic graphite used for battery cells currently comes from China, there should be tailwinds for Anovion’s value proposition to domestic cell makers coming out of the OBBBA ([source](https://thepostsearchlight.com/2025/07/16/anovion-still-aiming-for-2026-construction), `claim_e6124c6d86aed27b409c21b7`, `chunk_dab5e9bf52eac3bf75a16221`).

### q_company_9f7649ab93e553fce03a0293_discovery: "Club Car LLC" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- The Coalition seeks tariffs and duties on LSPTV products sold below market value and with the assistance of improper foreign government subsidies. Duties would be levied on any imports after Commerce’s preliminary determination beginning in November 2024. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_04cd20a229d56f2252148096`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- The attempt by Chinese companies to dump low-quality products into the American market ahead of any duties by the United States has escalated so quickly that Club Car and the Coalition have called for urgent action to impose retroactive duties on these Chinese imports. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_71f990e0f09ee679e1681d67`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- Textron Specialized Vehicles Inc., both based near Augusta, Georgia, in June asked the Biden administration to place a 100% tariff on golf carts and other low-speed, often battery-powered personal vehicles made in China ([source](https://www.ajot.com/news/us-golf-cart-industry-shows-china-tariffs-are-an-imperfect-tool), `claim_789199578e926cd239b78273`, `chunk_ef850dd356f213b54c86b911`).
- Club Car LLC and Textron Specialized Vehicles Inc., both based near Augusta, Georgia, in June asked the Biden administration to place a 100% tariff on golf carts and other low-speed, often battery-powered personal vehicles made in China ([source](https://www.ajot.com/news/us-golf-cart-industry-shows-china-tariffs-are-an-imperfect-tool), `claim_840406af53bab640033b1ab4`, `chunk_ef850dd356f213b54c86b911`).
- AUGUSTA, Ga. (October 24, 2024) - Club Car®, as part of the American Personal Transportation Vehicle Manufacturers Coalition, filed critical circumstances allegations with the U.S. Department of Commerce on October 22, 2024, to address a massive increase in Chinese imports of low-speed personal transportation vehicles (LSPTVs). ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_a8d977125ac3b77bc50a4a1f`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).
- For Club Car, which was owned by Ingersoll-Rand for many years but is now owned by private equity firm Platinum Equity, the golf cart supply chain is more diverse. In addition to the motors, speaker systems, plastic injection molds, chargers, brakes, clutches, and parts from China, key components are imported from a wide variety of countries. ([source](https://www.cnbc.com/2025/04/30/tariffs-target-trumps-second-favorite-set-of-wheels-the-golf-cart.html), `claim_f39c068f1231b2377ad228af`, `chunk_9ccef08723eccd0bdd8ca04e`).
- On July 10, 2024, Commerce opened an AD/CVD investigation into Chinese LSPTV imports, and on August 2, 2024, the USITC unanimously confirmed that these imports are indeed harming U.S. manufacturers. ([source](https://www.clubcar.com/en-us/our-company/news/fighting-back-against-surging-chinese-imports-club-car), `claim_f867123573295e4777ae1ef3`, `chunk_8cc135568219d23d4615b97c`).

- In a sweeping display of executive power, President Donald Trump announced a new baseline 10% tariff against all trading partners, in addition to a sliding scale reciprocal tariff he argued will combat the “asymmetries in trade relationships that have contributed to the atrophy of domestic production capacity.” ([source](https://www.thedailytimes.com/news/denso-staying-flexible-amid-tariff-talk/article_8ffbccc3-9f00-4311-bd30-a4e8eba623fb.html), `claim_192ffbcc37f0a45093c9cdb0`, `chunk_73411c76864c579b8645f702`).

([source](https://www.thedailytimes.com/news/denso-staying-flexible-amid-tariff-talk/article_8ffbccc3-9f00-4311-bd30-a4e8eba623fb.html), `claim_401644cb13bb7ce8a081c304`, `chunk_73411c76864c579b8645f702`).

- among the companies that could be most affected by the tariffs are Honda, Toyota and Mazda. - Honda disclosed the tariff burden while also reporting a 267 billion yen ($1.7 billion) operating loss for the last nine months of 2025 as it navigated shifts in U.S. electric vehicle policy. - Honda’s supply troubles included a semiconductor crunch in the third fiscal quarter, which is easing, Kaihara said. However, the next wave of risk is the rare earth supply chain, affected by China’s export restrictions. - The Sportage Hybrid will emerge from the high-tech Ellabell, Georgia facility, also known as the Hyundai Motor Group Metaplant America (HMGMA), a facility that can also handle electrified vehicles from Hyundai and Genesis. ### q_company_0070706649688b18a55fb394_discovery: "Kumho Tire USA Inc." Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- Kumho Tire produces approximately 3.3 million tires at its Georgia plant in the U.S., but most of the tires sold in the U.S. are exported from its Binh Duong plant in Vietnam (13.5 million tires). ([source](https://www.businesskorea.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=239145), `claim_0915091748e629db7a409711`, `chunk_75edbd46bcaa9cecb1e46ddc`).
- Particularly, 95% of its exports to the U.S. are manufactured in Vietnam, previously subject to only a 7.89% tariff. If the tire tariff rate rises to 25%, the importer's burden will increase by 178 billion won, which Kumho Tire may have to absorb significantly. ([source](https://www.businesskorea.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=239145), `claim_192921b42a95f749773e0435`, `chunk_75edbd46bcaa9cecb1e46ddc`).
- The U.S. has announced a 25% tariff on automotive parts, including tires, starting from April 3, which will result in higher tariffs on export volumes to the U.S. ([source](https://www.businesskorea.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=239145), `claim_4fe033b36839b650ecc7b703`, `chunk_75edbd46bcaa9cecb1e46ddc`).
- Kumho Tire is adjusting its global production bases. If reciprocal tariffs are imposed on Vietnamese export products, it plans to replace U.S. export volumes using its plants in Korea and Europe. ([source](https://www.businesskorea.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=239145), `claim_8a744570dfc3b5175b85ceb5`, `chunk_75edbd46bcaa9cecb1e46ddc`).
- South Korean tire makers such as Hankook Tire & Technology Co., Kumho Tire Co. and Nexen Tire Corp. are expected to post new record-high revenue in 2024 thanks to a drastic cut in tariffs on their US exports ([source](https://www.kedglobal.com/automobiles/newsView/ked202402060006), `claim_c264c7ee286aa87a8becbd07`, `chunk_19b93b3d1889636833f537b7`).
- It reduced tariffs on Hankook Tire’s products from 27.05% to 6.3% and cut those on Kumho tires from 21.74% to 5.4%. Customs duties on Nexen tires were lowered to 4.2% versus 14.72%. ([source](https://www.kedglobal.com/automobiles/newsView/ked202402060006), `claim_dfe262a3cf452756cdd32fed`, `chunk_19b93b3d1889636833f537b7`).
- Kumho Tire has been exporting tires produced in Vietnam, and Hankook Tire from Indonesia, to the U.S., achieving record performance. However, with high tariffs looming, both companies are establishing emergency measures such as expanding U.S. factories and adjusting global supply chains to minimize tariff damage. ([source](https://www.businesskorea.co.kr/news/articleView.html?idxno=239145), `claim_fa38b8310cf8ac3c22ef8192`, `chunk_75edbd46bcaa9cecb1e46ddc`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, facility_exposure_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.
