### q_company_1ccd8d549b6d5b76fe835826_discovery: "Lear Corporation" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- From the COVID-19 pandemic to President Donald Trump's tariffs on vehicles and their parts to halted exports of semiconductors out of China by manufacturer Nexperia, companies like Southfield-based Lear Corp., which makes seats and electronic systems, and Germany's Robert Bosch GmbH, which makes parts from brakes to electronic control units, say it's a daily battle to ensure the proper flow of parts needed to make the parts needed in the trucks, SUVs and cars Americans drive. ([source](https://www.detroitnews.com/story/business/autos/2025/11/14/lear-bosch-tariffs-nexperia-supply-chain-challenges/87263400007), `claim_2c8cb8f46ef93e498776a40a`, `chunk_eaad6a0342d2161720385f24`).
- Lear Corp. CEO Ray Scott emphasizes localization, alternative designs and onshoring as available responses to supply-chain challenges like tariffs. ([source](https://www.detroitnews.com/story/business/autos/2025/11/14/lear-bosch-tariffs-nexperia-supply-chain-challenges/87263400007), `claim_bcf7b57d375887b855cbc72e`, `chunk_eaad6a0342d2161720385f24`).
- Lear Corp. CEO Ray Scott said he is considering moving some production back to the U.S. as a tariff mitigation strategy for the automotive ([source](https://www.autonews.com/manufacturing/an-trump-tariffs-lear-moves-0326), `claim_d3b707b570d55a76df6b65dc`, `chunk_daee9ed1ddd8eb1c64bdb283`).
- "I'm all for an ambitious goal to build more here in the U.S. and supply great jobs," Lear CEO Ray Scott said at the company's innovation center at its Southfield headquarters. ([source](https://www.detroitnews.com/story/business/autos/2025/11/14/lear-bosch-tariffs-nexperia-supply-chain-challenges/87263400007), `claim_e50fd055327f628ed48672a6`, `chunk_eaad6a0342d2161720385f24`).
- Such tariffs are paid by companies who import products. The importers often pass along those costs to consumers. Lear is examining its supply ([source](https://www.forbes.com/sites/billkoenig/2025/11/12/lear-chief-provides-look-at-how-auto-supplier-is-navigates-tariffs-ai), `claim_f891ac307dbfd96b3d47ce93`, `chunk_a2be9addd1ffb8db7e0f7a45`).

### q_company_b5ea19d5054e315d972744db_discovery: "Lyle Industries Inc." Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- Lyle Company's tufting operation runs multiple gauge configurations — 1/10 gauge, 5/64 gauge, loop machines, high-speed creel systems, and beam systems. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_01b4f3d156b559f86bb9d527`, `chunk_2a528b9b808f6421583d8351`).
- Lyle Industries operates multiple tufting gauge configurations: 1/10 gauge, 5/64 gauge, loop machines, high-speed creel systems, and beam systems. Each configuration produces different pile density, surface texture, and yarn consumption profiles. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_24c559e6186a743aed258122`, `chunk_cb83751df51c931edf14a159`).
- Lyle Company — formally known as Lyle Industries — is an ISO 9001:2015 certified automotive textile carpet manufacturer headquartered in Dalton, Georgia ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_6cdc2d5ca5f36380cb2712c3`, `chunk_2a528b9b808f6421583d8351`).
- Lyle Industries specializes in tufting, precision cutting, and finishing and coating operations, supplying floor and interior trim systems for passenger cars, SUVs, light trucks, and Class 6–8 heavy commercial vehicles. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_71cc103ac01814844608cc94`, `chunk_2a528b9b808f6421583d8351`).
- All product categories ship from Dalton, Georgia facilities, with logistics access to North American assembly plants via I-75 and regional rail connections. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_74a73a3d7c9e23777aa65abb`, `chunk_2a528b9b808f6421583d8351`).
- Lyle Industries, an ISO 9001:2015 certified automotive textile carpet manufacturer founded in 1981 and headquartered in Dalton, Georgia. Founded by Lamar and Loutrelle Lyle, the company operates three manufacturing plants and produces up to 34.5 million square yards of tufted carpet per year for OEM, Aftermarket, and Accessory vehicle markets across North America. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_74e3c0a3a13140367faf5215`, `chunk_cb83751df51c931edf14a159`).
- Lyle Company produces these for both OEM fitment and aftermarket replacement kits. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_b0fbf09805afeda97fca5454`, `chunk_2a528b9b808f6421583d8351`).
- Lyle Company produces OEM-specification floor mats shipped direct to vehicle assembly plants and aftermarket replacement mats distributed through automotive parts channels. Material options include nylon-face cut-pile and polypropylene loop constructions. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_ba671d8881d4a7da6ac39cc9`, `chunk_cb83751df51c931edf14a159`).
- These capabilities collectively position Lyle Industries as a qualified supplier for programs at major North American assembly facilities operated by Ford Motor Company, General Motors, Stellantis, Toyota Motor Corporation, and their respective Tier 1 interior trim suppliers. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_c930f3f57150d8e593d94c60`, `chunk_cb83751df51c931edf14a159`).
- Lyle Industries operates out of Dalton, Georgia, a city recognized nationally as the center of U.S. carpet and rug production. The company runs three manufacturing plants in the Dalton area. Dalton's position at the I-75 corridor provides direct freight access to major North American vehicle assembly plants in Tennessee, Alabama, Michigan, and Ontario. ([source](https://www.lyleind.com/blog/lyle-company-automotive-textile-manufacturing-georgia), `claim_e98882135b09dd70ac6c647c`, `chunk_cb83751df51c931edf14a159`).

### q_company_ef38ad6c248a16d5d72da55c_discovery: "Mack Trucks" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- Trump Administration Imposes New Tariffs on Imported Trucks The administration announced 25% tariffs on imported medium- and heavy-duty trucks and parts, plus 10% on buses, starting Nov. 1 — aiming to boost U.S. manufacturing. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-freight-trucking-activity-7378398152019853312-0Lp1), `claim_3a3b94379d13a34a41ee9b86`, `chunk_408d2e0f79d22f9bbe18021e`).
- Companies with strong U.S. manufacturing footprints (e.g., Freightliner, Peterbilt, Kenworth, Mack) could benefit from reduced import competition. ([source](https://www.transportationtaxconsulting.com/truck-tariffs-take-effect-november-1-what-every-transportation-company-must-know-now), `claim_552e2a0c9b097175a1870c72`, `chunk_c4c9773c5ff57fae0ee34d48`).
- Therefore, our Great Large Truck Company Manufacturers, such as Peterbilt, Kenworth, Freightliner, Mack Trucks, and others, will be protected from the onslaught of outside interruptions. ([source](https://www.freightwaves.com/news/trump-sets-25-tariff-on-imported-trucks-raising-supply-chain-stakes), `claim_952d3ece693696a8f97d405a`, `chunk_2c6841ddf63437801bb61f7d`).
- Some estimates suggest that a 25% import tariff combined with a 12% federal excise tax (FET) could raise the cost of a Class 8 tractor significantly — from around $170,000 to as much as $200,000+ or even $224,000 in some scenarios. ([source](https://www.transportationtaxconsulting.com/truck-tariffs-take-effect-november-1-what-every-transportation-company-must-know-now), `claim_36db142c7f284fc7d2e13812`, `chunk_c4c9773c5ff57fae0ee34d48`).
- A 25% truck tariff aims to shield U.S. truck manufacturing, even though most trucks are assembled domestically. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-freight-trucking-activity-7378398152019853312-0Lp1), `claim_3093fac90c4ea3fce5776b55`, `chunk_0b9ab6ec0e68a28b86b9dcca`).
- Most heavy truck imports come from Mexico and Canada, and experts expect the new tariff to override USMCA provisions. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-freight-trucking-activity-7378398152019853312-0Lp1), `claim_b39025685c9eb04d646d77a7`, `chunk_0b9ab6ec0e68a28b86b9dcca`).
- The kind of impact it will have will hinge on whether the tariff applies to truck parts and if it supersedes the U.S.-Mexico-Canada Agreement. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/jason-miller-32110325_supplychain-freight-trucking-activity-7378398152019853312-0Lp1), `claim_e647a594d4974fac694ba373`, `chunk_0b9ab6ec0e68a28b86b9dcca`).

### q_company_6ad2cc466d4f49495289e32c_discovery: "Magna International" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- Magna International Inc. CEO Swamy Kotagiri said tariffs are "adversely impacting" the automotive industry. ([source](https://www.detroitnews.com/story/business/autos/2025/04/15/magna-ceo-swamy-kotagiri-tariffs-trump/83096064007), `claim_13f4c1fce2275bcd9320bc61`, `chunk_99be57d3ae8fce710ccad485`).
- Magna CEO Swamy Kotagiri said at an Automotive Press Association media event on Tuesday, April 15, 2025, at Magna’s headquarters in Troy tariffs on automotive imports is akin to hitting the industry with COVID-19, the chip shortage, and the Great Recession all at once. ([source](https://www.freep.com/story/money/cars/2025/04/15/auto-tariffs-trump-supplier-magna-international/83096063007), `claim_689339fa92436a891e6c9e9f`, `chunk_c0680ee359de5307852f5871`).
- As North America’s largest auto supplier, Magna employs more than 12,300 people in Michigan as of January and supplies parts to all the major carmakers. ([source](https://www.freep.com/story/money/cars/2025/04/15/auto-tariffs-trump-supplier-magna-international/83096063007), `claim_79f0619b43377b534464955f`, `chunk_c0680ee359de5307852f5871`).
- Reshoring production, meanwhile, would probably take much of Trump’s second term. ([source](https://www.freep.com/story/money/cars/2025/04/15/auto-tariffs-trump-supplier-magna-international/83096063007), `claim_954d09f255987bd223071921`, `chunk_c0680ee359de5307852f5871`).
- Lack of clarity on when tariffs will be applied, what will be impacted and material costs makes it even more difficult to plan production to fulfill contracts with automakers, Kotagiri said. ([source](https://www.freep.com/story/money/cars/2025/04/15/auto-tariffs-trump-supplier-magna-international/83096063007), `claim_a81ce1265a91587baee8ea81`, `chunk_c0680ee359de5307852f5871`).
- Trump earlier this month imposed 25% tariffs on vehicles imported into the United States, and 25% tariffs on certain auto parts are expected to go into effect May 3. ([source](https://www.detroitnews.com/story/business/autos/2025/04/15/magna-ceo-swamy-kotagiri-tariffs-trump/83096064007), `claim_adef9dee9293b51b8a594dca`, `chunk_99be57d3ae8fce710ccad485`).
- He added that Magna is “at the table with the policymakers and hoping to have a clear objective in the long term.” ([source](https://www.freep.com/story/money/cars/2025/04/15/auto-tariffs-trump-supplier-magna-international/83096063007), `claim_c5b88ec51df30662ef15f409`, `chunk_c0680ee359de5307852f5871`).

### q_company_a51a3f8338d12ca01a577774_discovery: "NVH Korea" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- Following months of negotiations, the United States and Korea reached the Korea Strategic Trade and Investment Deal in July 2025. After publishing a joint fact sheet on November 13, the U.S. government implemented the adjusted tariffs agreed upon by the two countries. ([source](https://keia.org/analysis/tariffs-on-korean-imports-implications-for-u-s-manufacturing-and-investment), `claim_1bf0c05afe4d48b594eccd15`, `chunk_b22ad065b6ece36ad2ca075f`).
- If Section 232 tariffs on unmanned aircraft systems and polysilicon take effect on September 3 and December 4, respectively, a portion of U.S. imports from Korea currently subject to U.S. MFN or preferential tariff rates under the KORUS FTA will face a 15 percent tariff, net of MFN duties. ([source](https://keia.org/analysis/tariffs-on-korean-imports-implications-for-u-s-manufacturing-and-investment), `claim_455d1b22c0ba9f4cb336f0a2`, `chunk_60c54951c92c5f349107c42e`).
- A 15 percent tariff, net of MFN duties, under Section 232 on the automotive sector applies to 214 automobiles and auto parts based on 8-digit HTS codes. ([source](https://keia.org/analysis/tariffs-on-korean-imports-implications-for-u-s-manufacturing-and-investment), `claim_50600dd28bd0edbda49a61d8`, `chunk_b22ad065b6ece36ad2ca075f`).
- The United States reduced its Section 232 sectoral tariffs on automobiles, auto parts, timber, lumber, and wood derivatives to 15 percent on an MFN-inclusive basis. ([source](https://keia.org/analysis/tariffs-on-korean-imports-implications-for-u-s-manufacturing-and-investment), `claim_74d501e4675c7bbac750a57d`, `chunk_b22ad065b6ece36ad2ca075f`).
- Consistent with the spirit of the 2025 trade deal between the two countries, the United States recently decided to apply a 15 percent tariff, net of MFN duties, to polysilicon and its derivatives, as well as unmanned aircraft systems and their components originating from Korea. ([source](https://keia.org/analysis/tariffs-on-korean-imports-implications-for-u-s-manufacturing-and-investment), `claim_ced355dd3bfc4b45b3bf00cb`, `chunk_b22ad065b6ece36ad2ca075f`).

- The plan also includes an offset to the tariffs for medium- and heavy-duty truck parts equal to 3.75% of the aggregate value of all trucks assembled in the U.S. from 2025 through 2030. ([source](https://www.ttnews.com/articles/paccar-tariff-market-share), `claim_355aed4afbc9464a7136a80a`, `chunk_cac5389d6027b09655741fa2`).
- Paccar began to feel the impact from the looming tariffs in Q2, as truck gross margins fell from 15% to 8.7% year over year. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/paccar-q2-2025-tariffs-section-232-investigations/754225), `claim_55d232225e79c315d641929f`, `chunk_b59e99d4fd6993003e17e66a`).
- President Trump's 25% tariff on all foreign-made cars. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/transport-topics_paccar-targets-35-n-american-heavy-duty-activity-7428197219754815488-wuyF), `claim_5de04b81f8814c61967ccbc7`, `chunk_8a1fb942284c96d87f30d3fa`).
- Deliveries were also down by 9,100 units, per the company’s Q2 presentation. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/paccar-q2-2025-tariffs-section-232-investigations/754225), `claim_642526d85ed12244ed82b7ae`, `chunk_b59e99d4fd6993003e17e66a`).
- Paccar, the parent company of Peterbilt and Kenworth, expects to seize a bigger U.S. heavy-duty truck market share once the Section 232 import tariffs on medium- and heavy-duty trucks and parts go live Nov. 1. ([source](https://www.ttnews.com/articles/paccar-tariff-market-share), `claim_70ab56b0428219e36ea933e5`, `chunk_cac5389d6027b09655741fa2`).
- Engines are manufactured in Columbus, Miss. ([source](https://www.ttnews.com/articles/paccar-tariff-market-share), `claim_7933d406feed64a342a339aa`, `chunk_cac5389d6027b09655741fa2`).
- Peterbilt Motors Company Kenworth Truck Co. https://hubs.ly/Q0434D-c0 PACCAR to benefit from 2025 truck tariffs ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/transport-topics_paccar-targets-35-n-american-heavy-duty-activity-7428197219754815488-wuyF), `claim_843f98a3bae0091552eb9f91`, `chunk_8a1fb942284c96d87f30d3fa`).
- The truck maker has also adjusted pricing strategies, adding a tariff surcharge in the U.S. and Canada. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/paccar-q2-2025-tariffs-section-232-investigations/754225), `claim_8da4f26bc55b22e6b3f4991e`, `chunk_b59e99d4fd6993003e17e66a`).
- The administration is being sued for over $150 billion in refunds following a Supreme Court ruling that deemed many tariffs illegal, according to Reuters. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/transport-topics_paccar-targets-35-n-american-heavy-duty-activity-7428197219754815488-wuyF), `claim_910de1e092dadbc45c6a77c1`, `chunk_8a1fb942284c96d87f30d3fa`).
- Paccar — and the rest of the industry — is waiting to hear the results of the Section 232 investigation into the imports of medium- and heavy-duty truck parts. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/paccar-q2-2025-tariffs-section-232-investigations/754225), `claim_921ecaa320e3ebf65dfef313`, `chunk_b59e99d4fd6993003e17e66a`).
- Imported Classes 3-8 trucks and parts received a 25% tariff as expected, but trucks that qualify for preferential tariff treatment under the United States-Mexico-Canada Agreement will be exempt. ([source](https://www.ttnews.com/articles/paccar-tariff-market-share), `claim_b40dd9d97dfb17a1f657cc19`, `chunk_cac5389d6027b09655741fa2`).
- Feight said the Paccar’s team has been proactively working with suppliers to ensure the company is sourcing the maximum amount of parts certified under the United States-Mexico-Canada Agreement, based on current standards. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/paccar-q2-2025-tariffs-section-232-investigations/754225), `claim_c3732608fd7c50d54aa942ae`, `chunk_b59e99d4fd6993003e17e66a`).
- Section 232 heavy-duty truck and parts import tariffs introduced in 2025 are expected to benefit PACCAR. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/transport-topics_paccar-targets-35-n-american-heavy-duty-activity-7428197219754815488-wuyF), `claim_d40037f16cf0a55dada417d5`, `chunk_8a1fb942284c96d87f30d3fa`).
- Peterbilt Motors Company Kenworth Truck Co. https://hubs.ly/Q0434D-c0 PACCAR to benefit from 2025 truck tariffs ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/transport-topics_paccar-targets-35-n-american-heavy-duty-activity-7428197219754815488-wuyF), `claim_e359f3c318435182a155460b`, `chunk_8a1fb942284c96d87f30d3fa`).

- Milan, Italy – Pirelli is remodelling its commercial strategy for the US market in response to US tariffs on imports of automotive parts, including tires. ([source](https://www.european-rubber-journal.com/article/2097398/pirelli-adjusts-supply-strategy-as-us-auto-tariffs-take-effect), `claim_3040532c42738b7cbad27ea6`, `chunk_b524a60392299a50da79d3e1`).
- Under the new tariff regime which took effect 3 May, a 25% tariff applies to car tires imported from Europe and Brazil, though there are exemptions under a trade agreement between the US, Mexico and Canada ([source](https://www.european-rubber-journal.com/article/2097398/pirelli-adjusts-supply-strategy-as-us-auto-tariffs-take-effect), `claim_558c18b2ce39f43b4e4fa6d8`, `chunk_b524a60392299a50da79d3e1`).
- The rest of the demand is served mainly through imports: 55% from Mexico and 40% from Europe and Brazil. Under the new tariff regime which took effect 3 May, a 25% tariff applies to car tires imported from Europe and Brazil, though there are exemptions under a trade agreement between the US, Mexico and Canada ([source](https://www.european-rubber-journal.com/article/2097398/pirelli-adjusts-supply-strategy-as-us-auto-tariffs-take-effect), `claim_6483c9351b16a85cf6465461`, `chunk_b524a60392299a50da79d3e1`).

- Rivian said the changed outlook centers primarily on the impacts of tariffs and the new GOP spending and tax bill President Donald Trump signed in July. - The GOP tax and spending bill rolls back and eliminates many EV tax credits and policies aimed at growing the fully plug-in vehicle industry in America. ([source](https://www.ajc.com/business/2025/08/rivian-hit-by-tariffs-and-ev-industry-headwinds-reaffirms-georgia-plans), `claim_ce8640715197aae07198697b`, `chunk_f6a02f1d125f272d990c515c`).

- President Trump announced today that the United States will impose a 25% tariff on imported medium- and heavy-duty trucks, effective November 1, 2025. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/chlo%C3%A9-meley-95186a152_skf-works-to-reroute-trade-flows-and-plans-activity-7321483294481551360-Qu9j), `claim_0429d137d1fb1fb9163293f3`, `chunk_b58112142d2e6221f8d066df`).
- The recent tariff escalation on October 10, 2025 (especially the proposed 100% tariffs on Chinese goods, export controls on rare earths, etc.) affected several industries across the world. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/chlo%C3%A9-meley-95186a152_skf-works-to-reroute-trade-flows-and-plans-activity-7321483294481551360-Qu9j), `claim_3bfd3f77aafacdec1557f876`, `chunk_b58112142d2e6221f8d066df`).
- The EU has increased its import quota from 47% to 50%, allowing slightly more non-EU steel into the market. Yet, tariff protections still apply once the quota cap is reached, meaning importers from India and the US continue to face tariff rates of around 25% beyond those limits. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/chlo%C3%A9-meley-95186a152_skf-works-to-reroute-trade-flows-and-plans-activity-7321483294481551360-Qu9j), `claim_a757c685ff8e90fa05002c12`, `chunk_b58112142d2e6221f8d066df`).
- The European Commission has announced plans to cut steel import quotas by nearly 50% and introduce tariffs of up to 50% on volumes exceeding the new limit. ([source](https://www.linkedin.com/posts/chlo%C3%A9-meley-95186a152_skf-works-to-reroute-trade-flows-and-plans-activity-7321483294481551360-Qu9j), `claim_b97fbeb6af2a2f925c40de3e`, `chunk_b58112142d2e6221f8d066df`).
- Rickard Gustafson, SKF CEO, told investor analysts in mid-July that SKF is compensating for tariffs with price increases that should keep the financial impact of tariffs neutral to the company's financial standing. ([source](https://www.post-journal.com/news/top-stories/2025/08/tariffs-costing-local-companies-resources-time), `claim_4a16ea1e92f621812173bc66`, `chunk_c8d99d9a0f6622cfad4b061c`).
- SKF officials say their customers will see price increases that reflect the increased costs of tariffs on the goods SKF manufactures and sells around the globe. ([source](https://www.post-journal.com/news/top-stories/2025/08/tariffs-costing-local-companies-resources-time), `claim_f94a734e8dd05c3af44cf802`, `chunk_c8d99d9a0f6622cfad4b061c`).

### q_company_aaa7f0307f6d78ed3a046f8c_discovery: "TE Connectivity" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- In the second quarter of fiscal 2025, the company initially estimated a 3% of sales headwind from enacted tariffs. However, following the 90-day pause for non-China countries announced in April 2025, the actual impact was reduced to 1.5% of sales. ([source](https://app.marvin-labs.com/agent-result/a197fb09-3a6d-11f1-a84c-0afff3e1c4b9), `claim_1a0038484e18781cd12d1bd8`, `chunk_f654a7db7bed9775bd2123c1`).
- The company's primary defense is its highly localized manufacturing strategy, with 76% of its production occurring within the same region as its customers and 90% of its supply chain sourced locally. ([source](https://app.marvin-labs.com/agent-result/a197fb09-3a6d-11f1-a84c-0afff3e1c4b9), `claim_65f06f1e2f07e4efb2c21b45`, `chunk_f654a7db7bed9775bd2123c1`).
- To recover these costs, the company implemented pricing actions representing approximately 2% of sales in its Industrial segment, successfully neutralizing the impact on net earnings. ([source](https://app.marvin-labs.com/agent-result/a197fb09-3a6d-11f1-a84c-0afff3e1c4b9), `claim_8cf73ff358ed9251c3e65616`, `chunk_f654a7db7bed9775bd2123c1`).

- TE Connectivity (TEL) has demonstrated significant resilience to the U.S. "reciprocal" and "Liberation Day" tariffs introduced in April 2025. ([source](https://app.marvin-labs.com/agent-result/a197fb09-3a6d-11f1-a84c-0afff3e1c4b9), `claim_eeb0dac1df8529ab972caef3`, `chunk_f654a7db7bed9775bd2123c1`).

- Industry experts have warned that automakers that cannot absorb the cost of border taxes themselves or pass it on to consumers will simply stop selling those models in the U.S. market. - Despite having a manufacturing plant in the United States, Volvo prices will inevitably increase over time to cover the cost of foreign-made models and imported auto parts, neither of which are safe from the new tax. ([source](https://www.volvocarshampton.com/impact-of-auto-tariffs.htm), `claim_66137df09afe9efdeff326de`, `chunk_c60fe06cb5ec16794eb99b6d`).
- Since 2022, Volvo has been hit by software bugs, supply chain snags, and tariff-related delays that slowed the rollout of its flagship electric EX30 and EX90. ([source](https://www.reuters.com/business/autos-transportation/volvo-cars-pauses-sales-some-cars-us-tariffs-pinch-profits-2025-07-16), `claim_74e71139cdbcf0eefb64c96e`, `chunk_eb769cddb11927be79079dca`).
- Volvo Cars is working overtime to cushion the impact of the auto tariff on U.S. buyers. While it is too late to stop the immediate influence of these new import taxes, the automaker is hopeful that the market will stabilize once it reduces its dependence on imported products. ([source](https://www.volvocarshampton.com/impact-of-auto-tariffs.htm), `claim_8e15b0ff461f0442f8cd0ac3`, `chunk_c60fe06cb5ec16794eb99b6d`).
- The company also aims to transfer much of its manufacturing to the United States in the near future to avoid future issues with import tariffs. However, it is still too early to tell the scope of this initiative. ([source](https://www.volvocarshampton.com/impact-of-auto-tariffs.htm), `claim_9020e4de887cf768de4e7ce8`, `chunk_c60fe06cb5ec16794eb99b6d`).
- There will be a Volvo tariff effect that reverberates through the entire supply chain. The 25% import tax applies to the entire automotive industry, including car parts and the raw materials used in Volvo production. ([source](https://www.volvocarshampton.com/impact-of-auto-tariffs.htm), `claim_91a7969696c79b9b4dfb12a2`, `chunk_c60fe06cb5ec16794eb99b6d`).
- Volvo, which releases quarterly results on Thursday, is one of the most exposed automakers to rising tariffs as the majority of its vehicles are produced in Europe or China. ([source](https://www.reuters.com/business/autos-transportation/volvo-cars-pauses-sales-some-cars-us-tariffs-pinch-profits-2025-07-16), `claim_9587b0cbd9182994482d48eb`, `chunk_eb769cddb11927be79079dca`).
- Import duties on vehicles made outside of the United States that were imposed on April 1 have made market conditions more challenging for foreign sellers to the U.S. market. ([source](https://www.reuters.com/business/autos-transportation/volvo-cars-pauses-sales-some-cars-us-tariffs-pinch-profits-2025-07-16), `claim_976f1f21ec9062d49e048aef`, `chunk_eb769cddb11927be79079dca`).
- Now that the U.S. government's 25% auto tariff has gone into effect, many are wondering – and rightfully so – what this will mean for the automotive industry. ([source](https://www.volvocarshampton.com/impact-of-auto-tariffs.htm), `claim_b99f289bb8afd817ace08799`, `chunk_c60fe06cb5ec16794eb99b6d`).
- To increase the utilisation of our Charleston plant and to reduce the effects of import tariffs, we will introduce local production of the best-selling XC60 SUV in the USA. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/lean-logistics/actions-taken-by-oems-to-curb-effects-of-tariffs/645543), `claim_c4a9a3f22181416a5c76dcd3`, `chunk_130ae533f5435d9a9676e6e6`).
- Samuelsson added that the OEM is starting to make its EX30 model in its Ghent factory in Belgium to reduce the impact of the US tariffs. ([source](https://www.automotivelogistics.media/lean-logistics/actions-taken-by-oems-to-curb-effects-of-tariffs/645543), `claim_d72e18132e9fb802822a6a9d`, `chunk_130ae533f5435d9a9676e6e6`).
- By the time deliveries began in 2024, EV demand had cooled, prices had spiked and new tariffs had kicked in. ([source](https://www.reuters.com/business/autos-transportation/volvo-cars-pauses-sales-some-cars-us-tariffs-pinch-profits-2025-07-16), `claim_ffdcda3b10c1cde516ada008`, `chunk_eb769cddb11927be79079dca`).

- The Mexican countervailing duties are also dependent on the exporter but are less than 2%. ([source](https://www.freightwaves.com/news/commerce-finding-on-van-imports-may-give-relief-to-beleaguered-wabash), `claim_64cb942e96b4e13a93515ff0`, `chunk_c5398b64175a7fcf6f77b991`).
- Wabash National, a manufacturer of van trailers, recently experienced another downgrade to its debt rating, though a potential market shift may be on the way due to new countervailing duties on imports from China and Mexico ([source](https://www.indexbox.io/blog/wabash-national-debt-downgraded-again-as-new-tariffs-on-china-and-mexico-offer-hope), `claim_73d7e9208baa1ab465f36623`, `chunk_8d8a80fa30c380e11ff79398`).
- In reducing its rating on Wabash to B- from B, S&P Global was matching the recent downgrade by Moody’s. ([source](https://www.freightwaves.com/news/commerce-finding-on-van-imports-may-give-relief-to-beleaguered-wabash), `claim_815f1c547384ac6b18889745`, `chunk_c5398b64175a7fcf6f77b991`).
- The complaint was brought to the Commerce Department by the American Trailer Manufacturers Coalition. It’s a three-company group: Wabash, Great Dane and Stoughton Trailers. ([source](https://www.freightwaves.com/news/commerce-finding-on-van-imports-may-give-relief-to-beleaguered-wabash), `claim_818fcb637fa3599947fcaa3e`, `chunk_c5398b64175a7fcf6f77b991`).
- The complaint was filed by the American Trailer Manufacturers Coalition, a three-company group that includes Wabash, Great Dane, and Stoughton Trailers. ([source](https://www.indexbox.io/blog/wabash-national-debt-downgraded-again-as-new-tariffs-on-china-and-mexico-offer-hope), `claim_a0e9f6420a3a8d14ad2c23dd`, `chunk_8d8a80fa30c380e11ff79398`).
- The countervailing duties would be levied on top of section 301 duties that have been in effect since May 2019. That levy is 25%. - Wabash National is facing a slowdown in activity across its business as customers delay purchasing decisions amid the uncertainty around the impact of tariffs, President and CEO Brent Yeagy said during an earnings call Wednesday. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/wabash-tariffs-q1-2025-earnings-losses/746952), `claim_b5f1732594e74ad795021558`, `chunk_e0722503cdac9b41eea4df55`).
- The countervailing duties to be applied to China will range from 82.3% to 128.7%, depending on the exporter. ([source](https://www.freightwaves.com/news/commerce-finding-on-van-imports-may-give-relief-to-beleaguered-wabash), `claim_d593f2784b105f765a2a9d4d`, `chunk_c5398b64175a7fcf6f77b991`).
- As a result of those headwinds, Wabash has reduced its full-year revenue outlook to $1.8 billion and decreased its earnings estimate to a loss between 85 cents to 35 cents per share. ([source](https://www.truckingdive.com/news/wabash-tariffs-q1-2025-earnings-losses/746952), `claim_fe55ba79978341ea73544947`, `chunk_e0722503cdac9b41eea4df55`).

- Yamaha already employs over 2,300 Georgians across various facilities, including its 1.3-million-square-foot factory in Newman ([source](https://www.wabe.org/yamaha-motor-corp-is-moving-its-u-s-hq-to-kennesaw), `claim_2803026ceae4b6dac3b6554b`, `chunk_e5504d6ec7b0fd5a92e00229`).
- Yamaha Motor Corporation is moving its U.S. headquarters to Georgia after half a century in California to increase profitability amid higher costs from tariffs. ([source](https://www.wabe.org/yamaha-motor-corp-is-moving-its-u-s-hq-to-kennesaw), `claim_62ad85959959a27748939e9b`, `chunk_e5504d6ec7b0fd5a92e00229`).
- Yamaha Motor U.S. intends to sell all fixed assets owned by the company in Cypress, California, including land, offices and warehouses. The Japanese company expects the move to help combat tariffs by improving efficiency and profitability in the U.S. market. ([source](https://www.ien.com/home/video/22962441/yamaha-ditches-california-for-georgia), `claim_7a2b8fa702affc5f5581a7b1`, `chunk_49c4b135910b6549c732e193`).
- The Japanese manufacturer, best known for its motorcycles and ATVs, is selling its over-25-acre site in California as part of the transition, including the land, offices, and warehouses. ([source](https://www.wabe.org/yamaha-motor-corp-is-moving-its-u-s-hq-to-kennesaw), `claim_c305fb56dadf1521776c7df2`, `chunk_e5504d6ec7b0fd5a92e00229`).
- Sina Golara added that Yamaha Motor Corp. is an international company, so part of its supply chain originates from outside the U.S. They make some parts, then move them to plants here for final manufacturing and sale. ([source](https://www.wabe.org/yamaha-motor-corp-is-moving-its-u-s-hq-to-kennesaw), `claim_cd1a31135277b25c35cd7c52`, `chunk_e5504d6ec7b0fd5a92e00229`).

### q_company_23ebfd09d6c7f37b2dc25904_targeted: "Enchem America Inc." automotive vehicles auto parts batteries critical minerals lithium graphite steel aluminum copper tariffs tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- On February 20, 2026, the Supreme Court concluded that IEEPA does not authorize the imposition of tariffs and invalidated the tariffs imposed under IEEPA. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_078b3480e7aa8c97ff90f77e`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- On February 10, 2025, President Trump published two presidential proclamations, expanding the Section 232 tariffs on imported steel, aluminum, and derivative articles. These proclamations eliminated existing exclusions and exemptions, both at the countrywide and importer-specific levels. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_1b7702104480962793473b75`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- USMCA carve-out: Qualifying USMCA parts for passenger vehicles may be exempt from Section 232 under HTS 9903.94.06 ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-tariff-rates-products), `claim_ab8006b2b58f303a3de84f0b`, `chunk_a6eba0765a54fb61c88f6b30`).
- Section 232 tariffs currently cover six product categories: steel and aluminum (50%), automobiles and auto parts (25%), copper (50%), timber and lumber (10-25%), and semiconductors (25%). Active investigations may lead to tariffs on pharmaceuticals, critical minerals, aircraft, drones, and robotics. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-tariff-rates-products), `claim_bb67d2002325c1eb95244bc1`, `chunk_a6eba0765a54fb61c88f6b30`).
- Tariffs imposed pursuant to Section 301 of the Trade Act of 1974 existed on some Chinese-origin lithium-ion EV batteries and non-EV batteries and non-lithium-ion battery parts. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_cd885fa15cb699bea6ade4be`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- In January 2026, the president issued a proclamation finding that processed critical minerals and their derivative products—including those essential for battery production, such as lithium, cobalt, nickel, manganese, and graphite—threaten US national security. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_d54fdfd53d7dbb608c297919`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- With the tariffs imposed in February 2025, President Trump was the first president to utilize the International Emergency Economic Powers Act (IEEPA) to impose a tariff on goods from Mexico and Canada and an additional tariff on goods from China based on a declared national emergency of an influx of migrants and fentanyl into the United States. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_f6f5afdc46ab148066f5f5e4`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).

### q_company_2ca807a86c0a7e4aeb4ae014_discovery: "SK Battery America" Georgia tariffs import duties supply chain

- Japan and South Korea, long-standing US allies and partners in battery cell production, face tariff rates of 24pc and 25pc, respectively. ([source](https://www.argusmedia.com/en/news-and-insights/latest-market-news/2674679-us-battery-costs-face-sharp-rise-on-tariffs), `claim_116579f362ba2c56e8cb5020`, `chunk_46a20c1adc606ab7eab9ab54`).
- With regard to synthetic graphite, another essential item for battery cell production, the US imported 115,778t in 2024, up substantially from 30,109t in 2020. Most of this came from China, at 74pc of the import market. This material now will be subject to 54pc tariffs, significantly increasing this cost for US battery cell producers. ([source](https://www.argusmedia.com/en/news-and-insights/latest-market-news/2674679-us-battery-costs-face-sharp-rise-on-tariffs), `claim_1afd08968fa38ea49f453fb7`, `chunk_46a20c1adc606ab7eab9ab54`).
- Trump policies are destroying the American battery industry. Here, the SK, Hyundai Joint Battery Plant is seen Friday, June 27, 2025, in Bartow County, George. ([source](https://washingtonmonthly.com/2025/07/22/trump-is-destroying-the-american-battery-industry), `claim_358942a640f3074fe7f4df46`, `chunk_2515ad4d719459e6bd2c9be3`).
- Battery cells imported into the US market face a sharp cost increase following the imposition of US president Donald Trump's new tariff regime. ([source](https://www.argusmedia.com/en/news-and-insights/latest-market-news/2674679-us-battery-costs-face-sharp-rise-on-tariffs), `claim_41c8647702d762dd68032a3a`, `chunk_46a20c1adc606ab7eab9ab54`).
- Last week, Donald Trump’s administration announced a staggering 93.5% tariff on Chinese graphite—a material essential to battery production. ([source](https://washingtonmonthly.com/2025/07/22/trump-is-destroying-the-american-battery-industry), `claim_4d1dea6056b26064abc606a6`, `chunk_2515ad4d719459e6bd2c9be3`).
- The move will send shockwaves through the already fragile battery supply chain and all but guarantee higher costs for U.S. manufacturers. ([source](https://washingtonmonthly.com/2025/07/22/trump-is-destroying-the-american-battery-industry), `claim_59ff59c6e019ed5c23ccb08f`, `chunk_2515ad4d719459e6bd2c9be3`).
- China not only dominates graphite mining but controls 96% of the global supply of processed anode-grade graphite, a necessary component of lithium-ion batteries. ([source](https://washingtonmonthly.com/2025/07/22/trump-is-destroying-the-american-battery-industry), `claim_658eb1b0b58d5766d8b44379`, `chunk_2515ad4d719459e6bd2c9be3`).
- Japan and South Korea, long-standing US allies and partners in battery cell production, face tariff rates of 24pc and 25pc, respectively. ([source](https://www.argusmedia.com/en/news-and-insights/latest-market-news/2674679-us-battery-costs-face-sharp-rise-on-tariffs), `claim_ce8b70bdf174045dfd1696c1`, `chunk_46a20c1adc606ab7eab9ab54`).
- China, by far the largest supplier of battery cells to the US market, is now subject to an effective 64.9pc tariffs, with the extra 34pc duty on top of other tariffs from previous administrations. ([source](https://www.argusmedia.com/en/news-and-insights/latest-market-news/2674679-us-battery-costs-face-sharp-rise-on-tariffs), `claim_f3a5d76d453d1c433103fce4`, `chunk_46a20c1adc606ab7eab9ab54`).
- Trump’s tariffs raise prices without incentivizing investment in domestic manufacturing. The 50-percent tariffs on imported steel have doubled the price of domestic steel, yet many companies still import because even inflated foreign steel is cheaper. ([source](https://washingtonmonthly.com/2025/07/22/trump-is-destroying-the-american-battery-industry), `claim_f503044b6fc0188ec03c2c99`, `chunk_2515ad4d719459e6bd2c9be3`).

### q_company_1a995c929c9b6b4f4eafd428_targeted: "Hollingsworth & Vose Co." LCD OLED displays PCBs printed circuit boards electronics tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Exemptions are confirmed via CBP guidance (HTSUS 9903.03.02–9903.03.11): USMCA-qualifying goods; goods in transit prior to February 24; certain steel/aluminum already under Section 232. High-tech sectors are not categorically exempt. Most electronics remain subject unless USMCA rules-of-origin are met. ([source](https://octopart.com/pulse/p/tariff-reset-pcb-workarounds), `claim_735ff471386127f113c1c130`, `chunk_0c1b9bf8d24806399bbcbc9f`).
- The regulatory landscape for electronics manufacturing underwent a seismic shift in February 2026, forcing procurement officers and design engineers to rethink their 2025 strategies. What began as a legal challenge to the International Emergency Economic Powers Act (IEEPA) has culminated in a complex "layered" tariff environment that directly impacts the landed cost of nearly every Printed Circuit Board (PCB), Assembly (PCBA), and semiconductor entering the United States from China. ([source](https://octopart.com/pulse/p/tariff-reset-pcb-workarounds), `claim_ba8aca5a7295fa640116fcd5`, `chunk_0c1b9bf8d24806399bbcbc9f`).
- Perhaps most concerning for the PCB industry is the looming November 10, 2026, "Compliance Cliff." On this date, 178 long-standing product exclusions, including those for high-volume 2-layer and 4-layer PCBs, are scheduled to expire. ([source](https://octopart.com/pulse/p/tariff-reset-pcb-workarounds), `claim_f9e68d349f423752482d275a`, `chunk_0c1b9bf8d24806399bbcbc9f`).

Limitations: current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

- For many China-origin bare PCBs under HTS 8534.00 and certain PCBAs under HTS 8473.30.1180, the additional Section 301 tariff is 25% unless a product-specific exclusion applies. - A 10% global tariff takes effect on PCB imports, while the existing 25% tariff on non-2/4 layer rigid boards from China remains in place (35% total). ([source](https://www.mclpcb.com/blog/important-tariff-update), `claim_286898d8d600e02809e1d659`, `chunk_bd5b124c929ced51695d707b`).

- Section 301 tariffs add an extra duty to covered China-origin goods on top of the normal HTS duty rate. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_4046c84280df4ec151bae7a0`, `chunk_8f16c1d4db7ae03fbbe4ce37`).
- A Section 301 tariff is a U.S. trade remedy duty applied to specific products from China following a trade investigation. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_55e4af2cde094061205b2225`, `chunk_8f16c1d4db7ae03fbbe4ce37`).
- In April 2025, the United States has implemented new tariffs on Chinese imports, including printed circuit boards (PCBs) and custom-made technical parts. ([source](https://www.icape-group.com/pcb-tariff-information), `claim_66281a1a384ea34abfc2a176`, `chunk_f47db68120770e70053ba980`).
- | LCD Screen | 8524.11.10.00 | 55% | ([source](https://www.icape-group.com/pcb-tariff-information), `claim_752b309f65f933ddecebb726`, `chunk_f47db68120770e70053ba980`).
- China-origin PCBs and some PCBAs can face a 25% Section 301 tariff unless a valid exclusion applies. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_8e2aee65feb8122db170c5a2`, `chunk_44199e7241fc2b339598dfec`).
- China-origin PCBs other than 2 & 4-layer FR-4 are now 37.5% ([source](https://www.mclpcb.com/blog/important-tariff-update), `claim_970f456300c35b39ee718f9a`, `chunk_bd5b124c929ced51695d707b`).
- Certain qualifying two-layer and four-layer rigid PCBs may use Section 301 exclusion code 9903.88.69 through November 9, 2026. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_aa446e038ce4a2832b86f632`, `chunk_44199e7241fc2b339598dfec`).
- CBP guidance states that duty-free de minimis treatment has been suspended for low-value shipments from China. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_b7f555ec21d1b5949b439e2b`, `chunk_44199e7241fc2b339598dfec`).
- For many China-origin PCB imports, the normal base duty may be Free, but Section 301 can add 25% unless a valid exclusion applies. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_c44086046e40f575acae26f4`, `chunk_8f16c1d4db7ae03fbbe4ce37`).
- For 2026 China PCB imports, buyers should not rely on duty-free de minimis treatment. Low-value shipments may still require proper entry handling along with applicable duties, taxes, and fees. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_da83285dc010ac8233908bbf`, `chunk_8f16c1d4db7ae03fbbe4ce37`).

### q_policyquery_2328ce6862090ff5a3dd9e62: LCD OLED display modules HTS classification Section 301 country of origin exemptions exclusions

- The additional duties imposed by headings 9903.88.01, 9903.88.02, 9903.88.03, 9903.88.04, 9903.88.09 and 9903.88.15 do not apply to goods for which entry is properly claimed under a provision of chapter 98 of the HTSUS, except for goods entered under headings 9802.00.40, 9802.00.50, 9802.00.60, and 9802.00.80. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_2db62176a3a97866b6687457`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- When importing goods put up in sets for retail sale (in accordance with General Rule of Interpretation 3) that contain articles subject to the Section 301 remedy, if the product that imparts the essential character to the set (i.e., the HTSUS provision under which the entire set is classified) is covered by the Section 301 remedy, then the entire set will be subject to the additional 25% duties. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_3d8a757d3fd1de4c3c363aa7`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- No, goods properly entered under Section 321 are not subject to Section 301 duties. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_6b3bc42d4dfef6fff55c9f9e`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- At this time, the additional duties imposed by the Section 301 remedy only apply to articles that are products of the People’s Republic of China (ISO Country Code CN). Imported goods that are legitimately the product of Hong Kong (HK) or Macau (MO) are not subject to the additional Section 301 duties. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_9eff683db24e1de8011235c7`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- For entries covered by an entry for immediate transportation, and with a country of origin of China, and a Harmonized Tariff Schedule (HTS) classification subject to Section 301 duties, such entries shall be subject to the duty rates in effect when the immediate transportation entry was accepted at the port of original importation ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_c87e17e8c48a27216f649f29`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).

- When importing goods put up in sets for retail sale (in accordance with General Rule of Interpretation 3) that contain articles subject to the Section 301 remedy, if the product that imparts the essential character to the set (i.e., the HTSUS provision under which the entire set is classified) is covered by the Section 301 remedy, then the entire set will be subject to the additional 25% duties. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_3d8a757d3fd1de4c3c363aa7`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- If the essential component of an article set (X line) is subject to a trade remedy, the applicable chapter 99 number should be reported on the X line with appropriate duty reported. If the article set V line is subject to a trade remedy, the applicable chapter 99 number should still be reported on the V-line, with duty reported as zero. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_8f72bfff798e40e613f96974`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- For entries covered by an entry for immediate transportation, and with a country of origin of China, and a Harmonized Tariff Schedule (HTS) classification subject to Section 301 duties, such entries shall be subject to the duty rates in effect when the immediate transportation entry was accepted at the port of original importation ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_c87e17e8c48a27216f649f29`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).
- At this time, the additional duties imposed by the Section 301 remedy only apply to articles that are products of the People’s Republic of China (ISO Country Code CN). Imported goods that are legitimately the product of Hong Kong (HK) or Macau (MO) are not subject to the additional Section 301 duties. ([source](http://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/section-301-trade-remedies/faqs), `claim_e7170c310e673c5bb5ab89a3`, `chunk_9f883d6865466f0c80d55de0`).

Limitations: current_policy_status_not_established, insufficient_evidence.

- Steel and aluminum were raised to 50% in June 2025. Automobile tariffs are 25% for most countries, with reduced rates of 10-15% for countries with bilateral trade deals. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-tariff-rates-products), `claim_4eda74e7cf2cf13d763175aa`, `chunk_a6eba0765a54fb61c88f6b30`).
- Semiconductor tariffs at 25% took effect January 15, 2026 and cover a narrow range of advanced computing chips. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-tariff-rates-products), `claim_ab57852d531c854bb119276f`, `chunk_a6eba0765a54fb61c88f6b30`).

- Section 232 keeps expanding to new product categories — drones are the latest, effective September 3, 2026. A proclamation signed August 13, 2026 imposes a 100% tariff on large, heavy, or thermal-imaging-capable unmanned aircraft systems and 25% on most consumer/hobby drones ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/section-232-tariffs-steel-aluminum-guide), `claim_c66b2477da2f14e96e652ea2`, `chunk_df1e7a5d377c4f163087fcf0`).
- Automakers, including Tesla, lobbied against the inclusion of EV related steel parts, warning that U.S. domestic production cannot meet current demand. - Section 232 tariffs are among the most impactful trade measures currently in effect. Unlike country-specific tariffs, Section 232 duties apply to imports from nearly every country in the world. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/section-232-tariffs-steel-aluminum-guide), `claim_e204a3a48f256047bff713a8`, `chunk_df1e7a5d377c4f163087fcf0`).

- On February 20, 2026, the Supreme Court concluded that IEEPA does not authorize the imposition of tariffs and invalidated the tariffs imposed under IEEPA. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_078b3480e7aa8c97ff90f77e`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- On February 10, 2025, President Trump published two presidential proclamations, expanding the Section 232 tariffs on imported steel, aluminum, and derivative articles. These proclamations eliminated existing exclusions and exemptions, both at the countrywide and importer-specific levels. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_1b7702104480962793473b75`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- USMCA carve-out: Qualifying USMCA parts for passenger vehicles may be exempt from Section 232 under HTS 9903.94.06 ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-tariff-rates-products), `claim_ab8006b2b58f303a3de84f0b`, `chunk_a6eba0765a54fb61c88f6b30`).
- Section 232 tariffs currently cover six product categories: steel and aluminum (50%), automobiles and auto parts (25%), copper (50%), timber and lumber (10-25%), and semiconductors (25%). Active investigations may lead to tariffs on pharmaceuticals, critical minerals, aircraft, drones, and robotics. ([source](https://gingercontrol.com/blog/section-232-tariff-rates-products), `claim_bb67d2002325c1eb95244bc1`, `chunk_a6eba0765a54fb61c88f6b30`).
- Tariffs imposed pursuant to Section 301 of the Trade Act of 1974 existed on some Chinese-origin lithium-ion EV batteries and non-EV batteries and non-lithium-ion battery parts. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_cd885fa15cb699bea6ade4be`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- In January 2026, the president issued a proclamation finding that processed critical minerals and their derivative products—including those essential for battery production, such as lithium, cobalt, nickel, manganese, and graphite—threaten US national security. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_d54fdfd53d7dbb608c297919`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).
- With the tariffs imposed in February 2025, President Trump was the first president to utilize the International Emergency Economic Powers Act (IEEPA) to impose a tariff on goods from Mexico and Canada and an additional tariff on goods from China based on a declared national emergency of an influx of migrants and fentanyl into the United States. ([source](https://www.morganlewis.com/pubs/2026/03/tariffs-and-trade-risk-in-energy-storage-projects-2026-and-beyond), `claim_f6f5afdc46ab148066f5f5e4`, `chunk_c08afd16edc799c5074ba66b`).

### q_company_5fd1cdbb7f4fedfb0fc5ec73_targeted: "Flambeau Inc." automotive vehicles auto parts steel aluminum copper tariffs tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- On March 26, President Trump signed a proclamation to impose a 25% tariff on all imported automobiles and certain automobile parts to address “a critical threat to U.S. national security,” according to a White House Fact Sheet. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_6ff61cf9e3aaed6db9d9a91b`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- The 25% tariff will be applied to imported passenger vehicles (sedans, SUVs, crossovers, minivans, cargo vans) and light trucks, as well as key automobile parts (engines, transmissions, powertrain parts, and electrical components), with processes to expand tariffs on additional parts “if necessary.” ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_41c16ac91af5a877c4949d08`, `chunk_0969af449067d814a4142531`).
- A new 25% tariff on imported auto parts officially took effect at 12:01 a.m. EDT on May 3. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_93a9fe5e94de3a55fe4f24a9`, `chunk_5c5c8c1128134c895d5d95df`).
- Trump imposed sweeping 25% tariffs on all steel and aluminum imports on March 12, which could potentially boost America’s steel and aluminum industries or raise prices on crucial ingredients used by U.S. manufacturers. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_b0ff163caee6a2ee7daff2c2`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- Trump has imposed new 20% duties on Chinese imports, reinstated 25% tariffs on global steel and aluminum, and applied 25% tariffs on certain imports from Canada and Mexico that do not comply with a North American trade agreement over the U.S. fentanyl overdose crisis. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_e8a959b206e155f24d2177ff`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- The 25% Section 301 tariff and most favored nations rates stack, so a commodity with a 5% duty would be subject to the additional 25% for a total rate of 30%. The document’s language also expressly provides for other applicable Chapter 99 duties to stack, along with AD/CVDs. ([source](https://usacustomsclearance.com/import-tariffs-updates), `claim_46aeb5824eda87940445ec4d`, `chunk_40a5765cb4c034e67d74c217`).
- Trump said in February that he intended to impose auto tariffs but later agreed to delay some auto tariffs after a push by the three largest U.S. automakers for a waiver. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_f28b2f29c3240c99d9f8236b`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).
- March 13: One-Month Exemption for Automakers, Steel and Aluminum Tariffs in Place Last week, President Trump granted a one-month exemption on recent tariffs on imports from Mexico and Canada for U.S. automakers. ([source](https://www.automotive-fleet.com/news/tariff-update-may-30-tariff-shifts-shake-up-auto-industry-as-legal-trade-battles-evolve), `claim_eb99eb7d816b88c3853bd978`, `chunk_28807caf1809cf89455cbfb6`).

### q_company_b7b4dc548795424b83c672ad_targeted: "Goodyear Tire & Rubber Co." automotive vehicles auto parts steel aluminum copper tariffs tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- In March, the Office of the U.S. Trade Representative claimed that Thailand's "trade surplus in sectors such as…rubber" was grounds for slapping higher tariffs on those imports. ([source](https://reason.com/2026/05/15/a-north-carolina-goodyear-plant-is-the-latest-victim-of-trumps-misguided-tariffs-and-costly-iran-war), `claim_25beeadeed0269796ae62876`, `chunk_fd93a281832748f0e7396fa0`).
- The Goodyear Rubber and Tire Co. announced this week that it would be shuttering its Fayetteville, North Carolina, plant that currently employs more than 1,700 workers. ([source](https://reason.com/2026/05/15/a-north-carolina-goodyear-plant-is-the-latest-victim-of-trumps-misguided-tariffs-and-costly-iran-war), `claim_328e96d966cd9723a32970ad`, `chunk_fd93a281832748f0e7396fa0`).
- Chinese passenger and light truck tires pay 37.5% effective duty before anti-dumping kicks in — 4% MFN base on HTS 4011.10, plus the 12.5% base reciprocal tariff (the Section 301 forced-labor tariff that replaced Section 122 on July 24, 2026), plus 25% Section 301 product duties. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/tire-tariffs), `claim_40fbca9a484e16756d03889e`, `chunk_49079f4d5649507092f9f69a`).
- Companies like Titan International, Inc. (TWI), an OTR Tire Manufacturer, and Myers Industries, Inc. (MYE), a Component Manufacturer, gain an immediate price advantage as tariffs on competing goods from Thailand (19%), Germany (10%), and non-compliant North American sources (25%) raise the cost of imports. ([source](https://koalagains.com/industry-tariff-report/chapters/40-rubber-and-articles/final-conclusion), `claim_479c86f803ad1eaf295680c3`, `chunk_df4b232b042d0764d316e03a`).
- The shift started with the original 2008 China AD order and accelerated after Section 301 added 25% in 2018. By 2022, every major tire brand had built or expanded Thai capacity: Bridgestone in Nong Khae, Michelin in Laem Chabang, Goodyear in Pathum Thani, Yokohama in Rayong, Sumitomo in Amata City. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/tire-tariffs), `claim_6bc1852183c8928c755796e6`, `chunk_49079f4d5649507092f9f69a`).
- That means American tire companies import rubber from places like Thailand, which has a climate well-suited to growing rubber trees and produces a lot more rubber than its domestic industries can consume. ([source](https://reason.com/2026/05/15/a-north-carolina-goodyear-plant-is-the-latest-victim-of-trumps-misguided-tariffs-and-costly-iran-war), `claim_70a201f92818d778750015ca`, `chunk_fd93a281832748f0e7396fa0`).
- Thailand exported roughly $4.5 billion in tires to the US in 2024 — more than China, Vietnam, or any single source. The shift started with the original 2008 China AD order and accelerated after Section 301 added 25% in 2018. By 2022, every major tire brand had built or expanded Thai capacity: Bridgestone in Nong Khae, Michelin in Laem Chabang, Goodyear in Pathum Thani, Yokohama in Rayong, Sumitomo in Amata City. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/tire-tariffs), `claim_8da1553fdb60304d7c0aacb0`, `chunk_49079f4d5649507092f9f69a`).
- Simply put: you can't make tires without rubber, and it is impossible to buy rubber that isn't imported—because rubber trees do not grow in the United States. ([source](https://reason.com/2026/05/15/a-north-carolina-goodyear-plant-is-the-latest-victim-of-trumps-misguided-tariffs-and-costly-iran-war), `claim_96f6277168dbc474f72961bf`, `chunk_fd93a281832748f0e7396fa0`).
- A prime example is The Goodyear Tire & Rubber Company (GT), a global manufacturer that now faces higher internal costs by paying tariffs on its own tires and materials imported from its facilities in Germany (10%) and Thailand (19%). ([source](https://koalagains.com/industry-tariff-report/chapters/40-rubber-and-articles/final-conclusion), `claim_a49afd2566ad709ecaf55ab5`, `chunk_df4b232b042d0764d316e03a`).
- The company said that it expected to receive $46 million in refunds after the Supreme Court ruled Trump's sweeping "emergency" tariffs to be unlawful. ([source](https://reason.com/2026/05/15/a-north-carolina-goodyear-plant-is-the-latest-victim-of-trumps-misguided-tariffs-and-costly-iran-war), `claim_b942f75650325a32e585d1e3`, `chunk_fd93a281832748f0e7396fa0`).
- The most significant negative impact is on companies with globalized manufacturing and sourcing strategies. A prime example is The Goodyear Tire & Rubber Company (GT), a global manufacturer that now faces higher internal costs by paying tariffs on its own tires and materials imported from its facilities in Germany (10%) and Thailand (19%). ([source](https://koalagains.com/industry-tariff-report/chapters/40-rubber-and-articles/final-conclusion), `claim_cffbfeed054621cf137d2729`, `chunk_df4b232b042d0764d316e03a`).
- Section 122 expired July 24, 2026, and was replaced the same day by the Section 301 forced-labor tariff: a 10% standard tier for most economies and a 12.5% tier for a 46-economy list — explicitly including China, Thailand, and Vietnam. ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/tire-tariffs), `claim_d023956d72d80f1a7dd039b2`, `chunk_49079f4d5649507092f9f69a`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure, incomplete_document, insufficient_evidence.

### q_company_2bc94a6bcf8d11a21a6cf4eb_targeted: "Vernay" automotive vehicles auto parts steel aluminum copper tariffs tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Since April 6, 2026, Section 232 duty applies to the full customs value of covered metal goods, not just the metal content. This raises the effective cost of many imports even where the tariff rate looks unchanged. ([source](https://www.jaggaer.com/blog/us-tariffs-direct-procurement-2026), `claim_1a0ba1a7c088afad26d7ad54`, `chunk_88cb344b27de48a9a10aebc7`).
- Full-metal steel and aluminum articles carry a 50% duty, derivatives above 15% metal content carry 25%, and automobiles, covered auto parts, and semiconductors carry a 25% duty. ([source](https://www.jaggaer.com/blog/us-tariffs-direct-procurement-2026), `claim_2657207f174991320ae4f438`, `chunk_88cb344b27de48a9a10aebc7`).
- Section 232 metals tariffs apply to auto components: after the April 6, 2026 restructure, articles wholly of steel/aluminum/copper carry 50% while derivative components substantially made of those metals carry 25% ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/tariffs-on-automobiles-parts), `claim_58231e50145689061b90faf7`, `chunk_6e50f5cf8ace5204b95d56e6`).
- effective April 6, 2026 (Proclamation issued April 2, 2026), Section 232 was restructured into two tiers: 50% on articles that are wholly steel, aluminum, or copper, and 25% on derivative products ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/section-232-tariffs-steel-aluminum-guide), `claim_612d4e26ec7b0db83bf8e407`, `chunk_df1e7a5d377c4f163087fcf0`).
- Section 301 (China) and the MFN base: a Chinese steel article pays 50% Section 232 + 25% Section 301 = 75% ([source](https://www.tariffstool.com/guides/section-232-tariffs-steel-aluminum-guide), `claim_fa3ef7b9495c4dbe69922617`, `chunk_df1e7a5d377c4f163087fcf0`).

Limitations: conflicting_sources, current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

### q_policyquery_6d9d7149a292548dbe1e9eff: steel aluminum copper derivatives Section 232 effective date status 2026

- Effective with respect to goods entered for consumption or withdrawn from warehouse for consumption on or after 12:01 a.m. eastern daylight time on April 6, 2026, until 11:59 p.m. eastern standard time on December 31, 2027, the applicable additional ad valorem rate of duty imposed under Proclamation 9704, as amended, and Proclamation 9705, as amended, for imports of products listed in Annex III to this proclamation shall be: (a) Determined by the product's current ad valorem (or ad valorem equivalent) rate of duty under Column 1 of the HTSUS (Column 1 Duty Rate) ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/04/09/2026-06960/strengthening-actions-taken-to-adjust-imports-of-aluminum-steel-and-copper-into-the-united-states), `claim_18a0487c1c895b1447efe8e6`, `chunk_75283d21b7138d2e9da58b1c`).
- Effective with respect to goods entered for consumption or withdrawn from warehouse for consumption on or after 12:01 a.m. eastern daylight time on April 6, 2026, the additional ad valorem duty imposed pursuant to section 232 on aluminum articles and aluminum derivative articles under Proclamation 9704, as amended; on steel articles and steel derivative articles under Proclamation 9705, as amended; and on copper articles and copper derivative articles under Proclamation 10962 shall apply to the full customs value of the imported product, regardless of metal content. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/04/09/2026-06960/strengthening-actions-taken-to-adjust-imports-of-aluminum-steel-and-copper-into-the-united-states), `claim_19a1c50c9bbd6da6f44d5d9c`, `chunk_75283d21b7138d2e9da58b1c`).
- The full-value ad valorem tariffs on aluminum and steel articles, certain copper articles, and certain aluminum and steel derivative articles shall be set at an ad valorem rate of duty of 50 percent, with reduced rates available for certain products from the United Kingdom given the ongoing discussions and for derivative articles made entirely with metals originating from the United States. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/public-inspection/2026-06960/aluminum-steel-and-copper-adjustment-to-imports-into-us-proc-11021), `claim_19b15d4858cf987e4ec3436a`, `chunk_1a2bb5e4a12b96f5ef689b0f`).
- (7) Effective with respect to goods entered for consumption or withdrawn from warehouse for consumption on or after 12:01 a.m. eastern standard time on January 1, 2028, the applicable additional ad valorem rate of duty imposed under Proclamation 9704, as amended; Proclamation 9705, as amended; and Proclamation 10962 for imports of products listed in Annex III to this proclamation shall be the rates proscribed in clause (3) of this proclamation. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/public-inspection/2026-06960/aluminum-steel-and-copper-adjustment-to-imports-into-us-proc-11021), `claim_19bce598244f5db27b1a8c01`, `chunk_4cd61df5f7c40f06c7ab6add`).

([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/public-inspection/2026-06960/aluminum-steel-and-copper-adjustment-to-imports-into-us-proc-11021), `claim_47a7a93413150467ec18f3ff`, `chunk_1a2bb5e4a12b96f5ef689b0f`).

### q_policyquery_4c8746c0d048e87aa446f11d: steel aluminum copper derivatives Section 232 country of origin exemptions exclusions

- The Department of Commerce previously concluded Section 232 investigations into imports of steel and aluminum articles in 2018 that led to the implementation of the Section 232 Steel and Aluminum Duties in the Exclusions Proclamations. These duties were extended to apply to imports of certain derivative steel and aluminum articles in Presidential Proclamation 9980 of January 29, 2020, as well as Presidential Proclamations 10895 and 10896 of February 10, 2025. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2025/05/02/2025-07676/adoption-and-procedures-of-the-section-232-steel-and-aluminum-tariff-inclusions-process), `claim_175c0ce8babe61c39ddd49dc`, `chunk_a037dcf64c9526b91a93c7af`).
- These Inclusions Proclamations terminated the aluminum and steel exclusions process as authorized in the clause 3 of Proclamation 9705, clause 1 of Proclamation 9777, and clause 2 of Proclamation 9980, as well as a number of country exemptions and alternative arrangements to the steel and aluminum duties implemented in subsequent Presidential Proclamations. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2025/05/02/2025-07676/adoption-and-procedures-of-the-section-232-steel-and-aluminum-tariff-inclusions-process), `claim_2026d41456a572fc50116cc4`, `chunk_a037dcf64c9526b91a93c7af`).
- On February 10, 2025, the President issued Proclamations 10895, “Adjusting Imports of Aluminum into The United States” (Aluminum Proclamation), and 10896, “Adjusting Imports of Steel into the United States” (Steel Proclamation), imposing specified rates of duty on imports of aluminum and steel articles and certain derivative steel and aluminum articles, respectively ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2025/05/02/2025-07676/adoption-and-procedures-of-the-section-232-steel-and-aluminum-tariff-inclusions-process), `claim_4174c03d0ac157b71543b32b`, `chunk_a037dcf64c9526b91a93c7af`).
- On February 10, 2025, the President issued Proclamation 10895 “Adjusting Imports of Aluminum into the United States”, and Proclamation 10896 “Adjusting Imports of Steel into the United States” which imposed a 25 percent ad valorem tariff on imports of aluminum and steel articles and certain derivative steel and aluminum articles, respectively. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2025/05/02/2025-07676/adoption-and-procedures-of-the-section-232-steel-and-aluminum-tariff-inclusions-process), `claim_9943ce1f3ee1846399bae517`, `chunk_a037dcf64c9526b91a93c7af`).
- Pursuant to the Inclusion Proclamations, this IFR makes conforming edits to remove the Section 232 aluminum and steel exclusions process regulatory provisions because that process is no longer in effect as of February 10, 2025, so those regulatory provisions are no longer needed. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2025/05/02/2025-07676/adoption-and-procedures-of-the-section-232-steel-and-aluminum-tariff-inclusions-process), `claim_74d9fcfab16516faf7067df5`, `chunk_893c8d7e0a1b573dcdf0ec07`).
- If the steel and/or aluminum derivative is classified in one of the new HTS classifications subject to Section 232 steel and/or aluminum derivatives duties on or after March 12, 2025, and the good is made exclusively from steel melted and poured in the United States, or exclusively made from aluminum smelted and cast in the United States, then the good is not subject to Section 232 steel and/or aluminum duties. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/232-tariffs-aluminum-and-steel-faqs), `claim_4af11c354620e958ded7bfd2`, `chunk_1025dee68f35b5ad25749848`).
- Updated 6/11/2025. If the steel and/or aluminum derivative is classified in one of the new HTS classifications subject to Section 232 steel and/or aluminum derivatives duties on or after March 12, 2025, and the good is made exclusively from steel melted and poured in the United States, or exclusively made from aluminum smelted and cast in the United States, then the good is not subject to Section 232 steel and/or aluminum duties. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/232-tariffs-aluminum-and-steel/faqs), `claim_4f1ae29dbbf1cf13c3dab9ee`, `chunk_1025dee68f35b5ad25749848`).
- No, there is no minimum amount of derivative steel or aluminum content provided for by Proclamations 10895 and 10896. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/232-tariffs-aluminum-and-steel/faqs), `claim_1fc6ca79d3595b17b2546a8a`, `chunk_c6c7c8bff61cc55ff7796347`).
- Articles and derivatives of steel and aluminum subject to Section 232 duties are excluded from the reciprocal tariffs; pursuant to heading 9903.01.33, HTSUS. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/232-tariffs-aluminum-and-steel-faqs), `claim_0c379e71e1426ddcfe19c36e`, `chunk_c6c7c8bff61cc55ff7796347`).
- Articles and derivatives of steel and aluminum subject to Section 232 duties are excluded from the reciprocal tariffs; pursuant to heading 9903.01.33, HTSUS. ([source](https://cbp.gov/trade/programs-administration/entry-summary/232-tariffs-aluminum-and-steel/faqs), `claim_7023dd9a1afa5221bdde9f11`, `chunk_c6c7c8bff61cc55ff7796347`).

- I found, under section 232 of the Trade Expansion Act of 1962, as amended, 19 U.S.C. 1862 (section 232), that aluminum, steel, and copper are being imported into the United States in such quantities or under such circumstances as to threaten to impair the national security of the United States and took action under section 232 to adjust imports of aluminum, steel, and copper articles and their derivative articles (collectively, metal products) so that such imports will not threaten to impair the national security. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/d/2026-06960), `claim_5bd10663d3ccda0bae5b76c7`, `chunk_1699afcedcec279ff909af7b`).
- Goods specified in Annex I-A, Annex I-B, and Annex III to this proclamation that are listed as articles or derivatives of more than one metal shall only be subject once to the respective duty rates established in clause (2), clause (3), or clause (5) of this proclamation even if the good contains aluminum and steel, aluminum and copper, steel and copper, or all three metals. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/public-inspection/2026-06960/aluminum-steel-and-copper-adjustment-to-imports-into-us-proc-11021), `claim_5c54efcdb3c9baf75ddc85cd`, `chunk_4cd61df5f7c40f06c7ab6add`).
- Effective with respect to goods entered for consumption or withdrawn from warehouse for consumption on or after 12:01 a.m. eastern daylight time on April 6, 2026, the additional ad valorem duty imposed pursuant to section 232 on aluminum articles and aluminum derivative articles under Proclamation 9704, as amended; on steel articles and steel derivative articles under Proclamation 9705, as amended; and on copper articles and copper derivative articles under Proclamation 10962 shall apply to the full customs value of the imported product, regardless of metal content. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/public-inspection/2026-06960/aluminum-steel-and-copper-adjustment-to-imports-into-us-proc-11021), `claim_6888c7ad226317f382fae180`, `chunk_75283d21b7138d2e9da58b1c`).
- Full-value tariffs on certain copper articles and certain aluminum and steel derivative articles shall generally be set at an ad valorem rate of duty of 25 percent, with reduced rates available for certain derivative articles from the United Kingdom given the ongoing discussions between the United Kingdom and the United States and for derivative articles made entirely with metals originating from the United States. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/d/2026-06960), `claim_6988d0551991c11c7de81859`, `chunk_1699afcedcec279ff909af7b`).
- (9) Goods specified in Annex I-A, Annex I-B, and Annex III to this proclamation that are listed as articles or derivatives of more than one metal shall only be subject once to the respective duty rates established in clause (2), clause (3), or clause (5) of this proclamation even if the good contains aluminum and steel, aluminum and copper, steel and copper, or all three metals. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/04/09/2026-06960/strengthening-actions-taken-to-adjust-imports-of-aluminum-steel-and-copper-into-the-united-states), `claim_a3d4e095151252b29b6cfc64`, `chunk_4cd61df5f7c40f06c7ab6add`).
- Effective with respect to goods entered for consumption or withdrawn from warehouse for consumption on or after 12:01 a.m. eastern standard time on January 1, 2028, the applicable additional ad valorem rate of duty imposed under Proclamation 9704, as amended; Proclamation 9705, as amended; and Proclamation 10962 for imports of products listed in Annex III to this proclamation shall be the rates proscribed in clause (3) of this proclamation. ([source](https://www.federalregister.gov/d/2026-06960), `claim_ba0917ec8e78f958e2dd8ee2`, `chunk_503d5d7e77c8283384608e1b`).

### q_company_1a995c929c9b6b4f4eafd428_targeted: "Hollingsworth & Vose Co." LCD OLED displays PCBs printed circuit boards electronics tariffs cost sourcing country of origin 2026

- Exemptions are confirmed via CBP guidance (HTSUS 9903.03.02–9903.03.11): USMCA-qualifying goods; goods in transit prior to February 24; certain steel/aluminum already under Section 232. High-tech sectors are not categorically exempt. Most electronics remain subject unless USMCA rules-of-origin are met. ([source](https://octopart.com/pulse/p/tariff-reset-pcb-workarounds), `claim_735ff471386127f113c1c130`, `chunk_0c1b9bf8d24806399bbcbc9f`).
- The regulatory landscape for electronics manufacturing underwent a seismic shift in February 2026, forcing procurement officers and design engineers to rethink their 2025 strategies. What began as a legal challenge to the International Emergency Economic Powers Act (IEEPA) has culminated in a complex "layered" tariff environment that directly impacts the landed cost of nearly every Printed Circuit Board (PCB), Assembly (PCBA), and semiconductor entering the United States from China. ([source](https://octopart.com/pulse/p/tariff-reset-pcb-workarounds), `claim_ba8aca5a7295fa640116fcd5`, `chunk_0c1b9bf8d24806399bbcbc9f`).
- Perhaps most concerning for the PCB industry is the looming November 10, 2026, "Compliance Cliff." On this date, 178 long-standing product exclusions, including those for high-volume 2-layer and 4-layer PCBs, are scheduled to expire. ([source](https://octopart.com/pulse/p/tariff-reset-pcb-workarounds), `claim_f9e68d349f423752482d275a`, `chunk_0c1b9bf8d24806399bbcbc9f`).

Limitations: current_policy_status_not_established, historical_or_future_measure.

- China-origin PCBs other than 2 & 4-layer FR-4 are now 37.5% ([source](https://www.mclpcb.com/blog/important-tariff-update), `claim_970f456300c35b39ee718f9a`, `chunk_bd5b124c929ced51695d707b`).
- Certain qualifying two-layer and four-layer rigid PCBs may use Section 301 exclusion code 9903.88.69 through November 9, 2026. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_aa446e038ce4a2832b86f632`, `chunk_44199e7241fc2b339598dfec`).
- CBP guidance states that duty-free de minimis treatment has been suspended for low-value shipments from China. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_b7f555ec21d1b5949b439e2b`, `chunk_44199e7241fc2b339598dfec`).
- For many China-origin PCB imports, the normal base duty may be Free, but Section 301 can add 25% unless a valid exclusion applies. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_c44086046e40f575acae26f4`, `chunk_8f16c1d4db7ae03fbbe4ce37`).
- For 2026 China PCB imports, buyers should not rely on duty-free de minimis treatment. Low-value shipments may still require proper entry handling along with applicable duties, taxes, and fees. ([source](https://www.ourpcb.com/blog/china-us-pcb-tariffs-2026), `claim_da83285dc010ac8233908bbf`, `chunk_8f16c1d4db7ae03fbbe4ce37`).
